  var __require = /* @__PURE__ */ ((x) => typeof require !== "undefined" ? require : typeof Proxy !== "undefined" ? new Proxy(x, {
    get: (a, b) => (typeof require !== "undefined" ? require : a)[b]
  }) : x)(function(x) {
    if (typeof require !== "undefined")
      return require.apply(this, arguments);
    throw Error('Dynamic require of "' + x + '" is not supported');

  // packages/diagnostics/dist/diagnostic-value.js
  function describeNonFiniteNumber(value) {
    if (Number.isNaN(value))
      return "nan";
    if (value === Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)
      return "positive-infinity";
    if (value === Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY)
      return "negative-infinity";
    throw new RangeError(`Expected a non-finite number; received ${String(value)}.`);
  }

  class DiagnosticValueError extends TypeError {
    details;
    name = "DiagnosticValueError";
    constructor(message, details) {
      super(message);
      this.details = details;
      this.details = Object.freeze({ ...details });
    }
  }
  function normalizeDiagnosticAttributes(attributes) {
    return copyAndFreezeDiagnosticValue(attributes, "attributes", new Map);
  }
  function copyAndFreezeDiagnosticDetails(attributes) {
    return copyAndFreezeDiagnosticValue(attributes, "details", new Map);
  }
  function copyAndFreezeDiagnosticValue(value, path, ancestors) {
    if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
      const firstPath = ancestors.get(value);
      if (firstPath) {
        throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${path} must not be circular; first seen at ${firstPath}.`, {
          path,
          issue: "CIRCULAR",
          firstPath
        });
      }
      ancestors.set(value, path);
      try {
        const symbolKey = Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value)[0];
        if (symbolKey) {
          throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${path} must use string keys; received ${String(symbolKey)}.`, { path, issue: "SYMBOL_KEY", receivedKey: String(symbolKey) });
        }
        if (Array.isArray(value)) {
          const copy = new Array(value.length);
          for (let index = 0;index < value.length; index += 1) {
            let descriptor;
            for (let owner = value;owner !== null; owner = Object.getPrototypeOf(owner)) {
              descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(owner, index);
              if (descriptor)
                break;
            }
            if (!descriptor)
              continue;
            const propertyPath = `${path}[${index}]`;
            if (!("value" in descriptor)) {
              throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${propertyPath} must use a data property; received accessor.`, { path: propertyPath, issue: "ACCESSOR_PROPERTY" });
            }
            copy[index] = copyAndFreezeDiagnosticValue(descriptor.value, propertyPath, ancestors);
          }
          return Object.freeze(copy);
        }
        const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
        if (prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) {
          const constructorName = prototype?.constructor?.name || "object";
          throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${path} must be a plain record; received ${constructorName}.`, { path, issue: "NON_PLAIN_OBJECT", receivedClass: constructorName });
        }
        return Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map((key) => {
          const propertyPath = `${path}.${key}`;
          const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key);
          if (!descriptor || !("value" in descriptor)) {
            throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${propertyPath} must use a data property; received accessor.`, { path: propertyPath, issue: "ACCESSOR_PROPERTY" });
          }
          return [key, copyAndFreezeDiagnosticValue(descriptor.value, propertyPath, ancestors)];
        })));
      } finally {
        ancestors.delete(value);
      }
    }
    if (value === null || typeof value === "boolean" || typeof value === "string")
      return value;
    if (typeof value === "number") {
      if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
        throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${path} must be finite; received ${String(value)}.`, {
          path,
          issue: "NON_FINITE_NUMBER",
          receivedNumber: describeNonFiniteNumber(value)
        });
      }
      return Object.is(value, -0) ? 0 : value;
    }
    throw new DiagnosticValueError(`Diagnostic value at ${path} must be JSON-safe; received ${typeof value}.`, {
      path,
      issue: "UNSAFE_TYPE",
      receivedType: typeof value
    });
  }

  // packages/diagnostics/dist/nodebraid-error.js
  var emptyDetails = Object.freeze({});

  class NodeBraidError extends Error {
    domain;
    code;
    cause;
    details;
    constructor(domain, code, message, options = {}) {
      super(message, options.cause === undefined ? undefined : { cause: options.cause });
      this.domain = domain;
      this.code = code;
      this.cause = options.cause;
      this.details = options.details ? copyAndFreezeDiagnosticDetails(options.details) : emptyDetails;
    }
  }

  var diagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    sinkFault: "nodebraid.diagnostics.sink.fault",
    faultReportingFault: "nodebraid.diagnostics.fault-reporting.fault"
  });
  // packages/diagnostics/dist/diagnostics-error.js
  class DiagnosticsError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "DiagnosticsError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("diagnostics", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

      super("kernel", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });

  // packages/kernel/dist/identifiers.js
  function nodeId(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "string") {
      throw new KernelError("INVALID_ID", "Node ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ entity: "node", receivedType: value === null ? "null" : typeof value }));
    }
    if (value.length === 0) {
      throw new KernelError("INVALID_ID", "Node ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ entity: "node", value }));
    }
    return value;
  }
  function edgeId(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "string") {
      throw new KernelError("INVALID_ID", "Edge ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ entity: "edge", receivedType: value === null ? "null" : typeof value }));
    }
    if (value.length === 0) {
      throw new KernelError("INVALID_ID", "Edge ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ entity: "edge", value }));
    }
    return value;
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/entity-values.js
  function freezeCanvasNode(node, id) {
    const type = node.type;
    const position = node.position;
    const ownedPosition = Object.freeze({ x: position.x, y: position.y });
    const size = node.size;
    const ownedSize = size === undefined ? undefined : Object.freeze({ width: size.width, height: size.height });
    const parentId = node.parentId;
    return Object.freeze({
      id,
      type,
      position: ownedPosition,
      ...ownedSize === undefined ? {} : { size: ownedSize },
      ...parentId === undefined ? {} : { parentId },
      data: node.data
    });
  }
  function freezeCanvasEdge(edge, id) {
    return Object.freeze({
      id,
      type: edge.type,
      source: freezeEdgeEndpoint(edge.source),
      target: freezeEdgeEndpoint(edge.target),
      data: edge.data
    });
  }
  function canvasNodesEqual(left, right) {
    if (left === right)
      return true;
    if (left === null || right === null)
      return false;
    return left.id === right.id && left.type === right.type && left.position.x === right.position.x && left.position.y === right.position.y && left.size?.width === right.size?.width && left.size?.height === right.size?.height && left.parentId === right.parentId && Object.is(left.data, right.data);
  }
  function canvasEdgesEqual(left, right) {
    if (left === right)
      return true;
    if (left === null || right === null)
      return false;
    return left.id === right.id && left.type === right.type && left.source.nodeId === right.source.nodeId && left.source.portId === right.source.portId && left.target.nodeId === right.target.nodeId && left.target.portId === right.target.portId && Object.is(left.data, right.data);
  }
  function captureCanvasNodeValue(value) {
    if (!isRecord(value))
      return;
    const id = value.id;
    if (typeof id !== "string")
      return;
    const type = value.type;
    if (typeof type !== "string" || !("data" in value))
      return;
    const position = capturePointValue(value.position);
    if (!position)
      return;
    const receivedSize = value.size;
    const size = receivedSize === undefined ? undefined : captureSizeValue(receivedSize);
    if (receivedSize !== undefined && size === undefined)
      return;
    const parentId = value.parentId;
    if (parentId !== undefined && (typeof parentId !== "string" || parentId.length === 0))
      return;
    if (id.length === 0)
      return;
    return Object.freeze({
      id: nodeId(id),
      type,
      position,
      ...size === undefined ? {} : { size },
      ...parentId === undefined ? {} : { parentId: nodeId(parentId) },
      get data() {
        return value.data;
      }
    });
  }
  function captureCanvasEdgeValue(value) {
    if (!isRecord(value))
      return;
    const id = value.id;
    if (typeof id !== "string")
      return;
    const type = value.type;
    if (typeof type !== "string" || !("data" in value))
      return;
    const source = captureEndpointValue(value.source);
    if (!source)
      return;
    const target = captureEndpointValue(value.target);
    if (!target || id.length === 0)
      return;
    return Object.freeze({
      id: edgeId(id),
      type,
      source,
      target,
      get data() {
        return value.data;
      }
    });
  }
  function isRecord(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }
  function freezeEdgeEndpoint(endpoint) {
    const nodeId = endpoint.nodeId;
    const portId = endpoint.portId;
    return Object.freeze({
      nodeId,
      ...portId === undefined ? {} : { portId }
    });
  }
  function capturePointValue(value) {
    if (!isRecord(value))
      return;
    const x = value.x;
    if (typeof x !== "number")
      return;
    const y = value.y;
    if (typeof y !== "number")
      return;
    return Object.freeze({ x, y });
  }
  function captureSizeValue(value) {
    if (!isRecord(value))
      return;
    const width = value.width;
    if (typeof width !== "number")
      return;
    const height = value.height;
    if (typeof height !== "number")
      return;
    return Object.freeze({ width, height });
  }
  function captureEndpointValue(value) {
    if (!isRecord(value))
      return;
    const id = value.nodeId;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
      return;
    const portId = value.portId;
    if (portId !== undefined && typeof portId !== "string")
      return;
    return Object.freeze({ nodeId: nodeId(id), ...portId === undefined ? {} : { portId } });
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/change-set.js
  function applyChangeSetToDraft(changeSet, direction, draftNodes, draftEdges, nodeWriter, edgeWriter) {
    const changes = validateChangeSet(changeSet, direction);
    for (const change of changes) {
      if (change.entity === "node") {
        const expected = direction === "forward" ? change.before : change.after;
        const actual = draftNodes.get(change.id) ?? null;
        if (!canvasNodesEqual(actual, expected))
          throwChangeSetConflict(change, direction, expected, actual);
      } else {
        const expected = direction === "forward" ? change.before : change.after;
        const actual = draftEdges.get(change.id) ?? null;
        if (!canvasEdgesEqual(actual, expected))
          throwChangeSetConflict(change, direction, expected, actual);
      }
    }
    const orderedChanges = direction === "forward" ? changes : [...changes].reverse();
    for (const change of orderedChanges) {
      const source = direction === "forward" ? change.before : change.after;
      const target = direction === "forward" ? change.after : change.before;
      const writer = change.entity === "node" ? nodeWriter : edgeWriter;
      if (source === null && target !== null) {
        writer.add(target);
      } else if (source !== null && target === null) {
        writer.remove(change.id);
      } else if (target !== null) {
        writer.replace(change.id, target);
      }
    }
  }
  function validateChangeSet(changeSet, direction) {
    if (direction !== "forward" && direction !== "reverse") {
      if (typeof direction !== "string") {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set direction must be forward or reverse.", {
          issue: "INVALID_DIRECTION",
          receivedType: direction === null ? "null" : typeof direction
        });
      }
      throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set direction must be forward or reverse.", {
        issue: "INVALID_DIRECTION",
        direction
      });
    }
    if (!isRecord(changeSet)) {
      throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set must be an object.", {});
    }
    const beforeRevision = changeSet.beforeRevision;
    const revision = changeSet.revision;
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(beforeRevision) || beforeRevision < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(revision) || revision !== beforeRevision + 1) {
      throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set revisions must be adjacent non-negative safe integers.", {
        issue: "INVALID_REVISIONS",
        beforeRevision: describeInvalidRevision(beforeRevision),
        revision: describeInvalidRevision(revision)
      });
    }
    const changes = changeSet.changes;
    if (!Array.isArray(changes)) {
      throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set must contain at least one change.", {});
    }
    const length = changes.length;
    if (length === 0) {
      throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set must contain at least one change.", {});
    }
    const identities = new Set;
    const accepted = [];
    for (const index of Array.prototype.keys.call({ length })) {
      const change = changes[index];
      if (!isRecord(change)) {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change must identify a Node or Edge.", { index });
      }
      const entityKind = change.entity;
      if (entityKind !== "node" && entityKind !== "edge") {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change must identify a Node or Edge.", { index });
      }
      const id = change.id;
      if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0) {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change entity ID must be a non-empty string.", { index });
      }
      const identity = `${entityKind}\x00${id}`;
      if (identities.has(identity)) {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change Set must not contain duplicate entity changes.", {
          index,
          entity: entityKind,
          id
        });
      }
      identities.add(identity);
      const before = change.before;
      const after = change.after;
      if (before === null && after === null) {
        throwInvalidChangeSet("Change must contain a before or after entity.", { index });
      }
      const captureSide = (entity, side) => {
        if (entity === null)
          return null;
        const captured = entityKind === "node" ? captureCanvasNodeValue(entity) : captureCanvasEdgeValue(entity);
        if (!captured || captured.id !== id) {
          throwInvalidChangeSet("Change entity must match its kind and ID.", {
            index,
            side,
            entity: entityKind,
            id
          });
        }
        return captured;
      };
      const ownedBefore = captureSide(before, "before");
      const ownedAfter = captureSide(after, "after");
      accepted.push(Object.freeze({ entity: entityKind, id, before: ownedBefore, after: ownedAfter }));
    }
    return accepted;
  }
  function throwChangeSetConflict(change, direction, expected, actual) {
    throw new KernelError("CHANGE_SET_CONFLICT", `Change Set source does not match ${change.entity} "${change.id}".`, Object.freeze({
      entity: change.entity,
      id: change.id,
      direction,
      expectedState: expected === null ? "missing" : "present",
      actualState: actual === null ? "missing" : "present"
    }));
  }
  function throwInvalidChangeSet(message, details) {
    throw new KernelError("INVALID_CHANGE_SET", message, Object.freeze({ ...details }));
  }
  function describeInvalidRevision(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "number") {
      return Object.freeze({
        receivedType: value === null ? "null" : typeof value
      });
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
      return Object.freeze({ receivedNumber: describeNonFiniteNumber(value) });
    }
    return Object.freeze({ value });
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/ordering.js
  function compareIds(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/graph-index.js
  class GraphIndex {
    #incoming = new Map;
    #outgoing = new Map;
    #children = new Map;
    constructor(nodes, edges) {
      for (const node of nodes.values()) {
        if (node.parentId !== undefined)
          append(this.#children, node.parentId, node);
      }
      for (const edge of edges.values()) {
        append(this.#outgoing, edge.source.nodeId, edge);
        append(this.#incoming, edge.target.nodeId, edge);
      }
      sortBuckets(this.#children);
      sortBuckets(this.#incoming);
      sortBuckets(this.#outgoing);
    }
    addNode(node) {
      if (node.parentId !== undefined)
        insertSorted(this.#children, node.parentId, node);
    }
    replaceNode(before, after) {
      if (before.parentId !== undefined)
        removeById(this.#children, before.parentId, before.id);
      if (after.parentId !== undefined)
        insertSorted(this.#children, after.parentId, after);
    }
    removeNode(node) {
      if (node.parentId !== undefined)
        removeById(this.#children, node.parentId, node.id);
    }
    addEdge(edge) {
      insertSorted(this.#outgoing, edge.source.nodeId, edge);
      insertSorted(this.#incoming, edge.target.nodeId, edge);
    }
    replaceEdge(before, after) {
      this.removeEdge(before);
      this.addEdge(after);
    }
    removeEdge(edge) {
      removeById(this.#outgoing, edge.source.nodeId, edge.id);
      removeById(this.#incoming, edge.target.nodeId, edge.id);
    }
    getIncoming(nodeId) {
      return frozenCopy(this.#incoming.get(nodeId));
    }
    getOutgoing(nodeId) {
      return frozenCopy(this.#outgoing.get(nodeId));
    }
    getIncident(nodeId) {
      return Object.freeze(mergeById(this.#incoming.get(nodeId) ?? [], this.#outgoing.get(nodeId) ?? []));
    }
    getChildren(parentId) {
      return frozenCopy(this.#children.get(parentId));
    }
  }
  function createCanvasQuery(nodes, edges, index, assertOpen = () => {}) {
    const requireNode = (id) => {
      if (!nodes.has(id)) {
        throw new KernelError("ENTITY_NOT_FOUND", `Node "${id}" does not exist.`, Object.freeze({ entity: "node", id }));
      }
    };
    return Object.freeze({
      getNode(id) {
        assertOpen();
        return nodes.get(id);
      },
      getEdge(id) {
        assertOpen();
        return edges.get(id);
      },
      getIncomingEdges(id) {
        assertOpen();
        requireNode(id);
        return index.getIncoming(id);
      },
      getOutgoingEdges(id) {
        assertOpen();
        requireNode(id);
        return index.getOutgoing(id);
      },
      getIncidentEdges(id) {
        assertOpen();
        requireNode(id);
        return index.getIncident(id);
      },
      getChildren(id) {
        assertOpen();
        requireNode(id);
        return index.getChildren(id);
      }
    });
  }
  function append(buckets, key, value) {
    const bucket = buckets.get(key);
    if (bucket === undefined)
      buckets.set(key, [value]);
    else
      bucket.push(value);
  }
  function sortBuckets(buckets) {
    for (const bucket of buckets.values())
      bucket.sort((left, right) => compareIds(left.id, right.id));
  }
  function insertSorted(buckets, key, value) {
    const bucket = buckets.get(key);
    if (bucket === undefined) {
      buckets.set(key, [value]);
      return;
    }
    let low = 0;
    let high = bucket.length;
    while (low < high) {
      const middle = low + high >>> 1;
      if (compareIds(bucket[middle].id, value.id) < 0)
        low = middle + 1;
      else
        high = middle;
    }
    bucket.splice(low, 0, value);
  }
  function removeById(buckets, key, id) {
    const bucket = buckets.get(key);
    if (bucket === undefined)
      return;
    const index = bucket.findIndex((value) => value.id === id);
    if (index >= 0)
      bucket.splice(index, 1);
    if (bucket.length === 0)
      buckets.delete(key);
  }
  function frozenCopy(values) {
    return Object.freeze(values === undefined ? [] : [...values]);
  }
  function mergeById(left, right) {
    const merged = [];
    let leftIndex = 0;
    let rightIndex = 0;
    while (leftIndex < left.length || rightIndex < right.length) {
      const leftValue = left[leftIndex];
      const rightValue = right[rightIndex];
      if (leftValue === undefined) {
        merged.push(rightValue);
        rightIndex += 1;
      } else if (rightValue === undefined) {
        merged.push(leftValue);
        leftIndex += 1;
      } else {
        const comparison = compareIds(leftValue.id, rightValue.id);
        if (comparison <= 0) {
          merged.push(leftValue);
          leftIndex += 1;
        }
        if (comparison >= 0) {
          if (comparison > 0)
            merged.push(rightValue);
          rightIndex += 1;
        }
      }
    }
    return merged;
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/canvas-view.js
  function createCanvasView(revision, nodes, edges) {
    const sortedNodes = Object.freeze([...nodes.values()].sort((left, right) => compareIds(left.id, right.id)));
    const sortedEdges = Object.freeze([...edges.values()].sort((left, right) => compareIds(left.id, right.id)));
    const snapshot = Object.freeze({
      revision,
      nodes: sortedNodes,
      edges: sortedEdges
    });
    const index = new GraphIndex(nodes, edges);
    return Object.freeze({ snapshot, query: createCanvasQuery(nodes, edges, index) });
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/entity-collection.js
  function createEntityWriter(options) {
    const label = options.kind === "node" ? "Node" : "Edge";
    return Object.freeze({
      add(entity) {
        options.assertOpen();
        const id = entity.id;
        if (options.draft.has(id)) {
          throw new KernelError("ENTITY_ALREADY_EXISTS", `${label} "${id}" already exists.`, Object.freeze({ entity: options.kind, id }));
        }
        const frozen = options.freeze(entity, id);
        options.draft.set(id, frozen);
        options.onAdd(frozen);
        options.touchedIds.add(id);
      },
      replace(id, entity) {
        options.assertOpen();
        const before = options.draft.get(id);
        if (before === undefined) {
          throw new KernelError("ENTITY_NOT_FOUND", `${label} "${id}" does not exist.`, Object.freeze({ entity: options.kind, id }));
        }
        const actualId = entity.id;
        if (actualId !== id) {
          throw new KernelError("ENTITY_ID_MISMATCH", `Replacement ${label} ID "${actualId}" does not match "${id}".`, Object.freeze({ entity: options.kind, id, actualId }));
        }
        const frozen = options.freeze(entity, actualId);
        options.draft.set(id, frozen);
        options.onReplace(before, frozen);
        options.touchedIds.add(id);
      },
      remove(id) {
        options.assertOpen();
        const entity = options.draft.get(id);
        if (entity === undefined) {
          throw new KernelError("ENTITY_NOT_FOUND", `${label} "${id}" does not exist.`, Object.freeze({ entity: options.kind, id }));
        }
        options.draft.delete(id);
        options.onRemove(entity);
        options.touchedIds.add(id);
      }
    });
  }
  function collectEntityChanges(kind, before, after, touchedIds, equal) {
    return [...touchedIds].map((id) => ({ id, before: before.get(id) ?? null, after: after.get(id) ?? null })).filter((change) => !equal(change.before, change.after)).sort((left, right) => compareIds(left.id, right.id)).map((change) => Object.freeze({ entity: kind, ...change }));
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/graph-validation.js
  function validateGraph(nodes, edges) {
    const issues = [];
    for (const node of nodes.values()) {
      for (const coordinate of ["x", "y"]) {
        const value = node.position[coordinate];
        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
          issues.push(Object.freeze({ code: "INVALID_POSITION", nodeId: node.id, coordinate, value }));
        }
      }
      if (node.size !== undefined) {
        for (const dimension of ["width", "height"]) {
          const value = node.size[dimension];
          if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {
            issues.push(Object.freeze({ code: "INVALID_SIZE", nodeId: node.id, dimension, value }));
          }
        }
      }
      if (node.parentId !== undefined && !nodes.has(node.parentId)) {
        issues.push(Object.freeze({ code: "MISSING_PARENT", nodeId: node.id, parentId: node.parentId }));
      }
    }
    for (const edge of edges.values()) {
      for (const endpoint of ["source", "target"]) {
        const nodeId = edge[endpoint].nodeId;
        if (!nodes.has(nodeId)) {
          issues.push(Object.freeze({ code: "MISSING_EDGE_ENDPOINT", edgeId: edge.id, endpoint, nodeId }));
        }
      }
    }
    issues.push(...findParentCycles(nodes));
    return issues.sort(compareGraphIssues);
  }
  function describeGraphIssues(issues) {
    return issues.map((issue) => {
      switch (issue.code) {
        case "MISSING_EDGE_ENDPOINT":
          return {
            code: issue.code,
            edgeId: issue.edgeId,
            endpoint: issue.endpoint,
            nodeId: issue.nodeId
          };
        case "MISSING_PARENT":
          return { code: issue.code, nodeId: issue.nodeId, parentId: issue.parentId };
        case "PARENT_CYCLE":
          return { code: issue.code, nodeIds: issue.nodeIds };
        case "INVALID_POSITION":
          return {
            code: issue.code,
            nodeId: issue.nodeId,
            coordinate: issue.coordinate,
            receivedNumber: describeNonFiniteNumber(issue.value)
          };
        case "INVALID_SIZE":
          return {
            code: issue.code,
            nodeId: issue.nodeId,
            dimension: issue.dimension,
            ...Number.isFinite(issue.value) ? { value: issue.value } : { receivedNumber: describeNonFiniteNumber(issue.value) }
          };
      }
    });
  }
  function findParentCycles(nodes) {
    const completed = new Set;
    const cycles = [];
    for (const startId of [...nodes.keys()].sort(compareIds)) {
      if (completed.has(startId))
        continue;
      const path = [];
      const pathIndexes = new Map;
      let currentId = startId;
      while (currentId !== undefined && nodes.has(currentId) && !completed.has(currentId)) {
        const cycleStart = pathIndexes.get(currentId);
        if (cycleStart !== undefined) {
          const nodeIds = Object.freeze(path.slice(cycleStart).sort(compareIds));
          cycles.push(Object.freeze({ code: "PARENT_CYCLE", nodeIds }));
          break;
        }
        pathIndexes.set(currentId, path.length);
        path.push(currentId);
        currentId = nodes.get(currentId)?.parentId;
      }
      for (const nodeId of path)
        completed.add(nodeId);
    }
    return cycles;
  }
  function compareGraphIssues(left, right) {
    const codeOrder = graphIssueOrder[left.code] - graphIssueOrder[right.code];
    if (codeOrder !== 0)
      return codeOrder;
    switch (left.code) {
      case "MISSING_EDGE_ENDPOINT":
        if (right.code !== left.code)
          return 0;
        return compareIds(left.edgeId, right.edgeId) || compareIds(left.endpoint, right.endpoint);
      case "MISSING_PARENT":
        if (right.code !== left.code)
          return 0;
        return compareIds(left.nodeId, right.nodeId);
      case "PARENT_CYCLE":
        if (right.code !== left.code)
          return 0;
        return compareIds(left.nodeIds.join("\x00"), right.nodeIds.join("\x00"));
      case "INVALID_POSITION":
        if (right.code !== left.code)
          return 0;
        return compareIds(left.nodeId, right.nodeId) || compareIds(left.coordinate, right.coordinate);
      case "INVALID_SIZE":
        if (right.code !== left.code)
          return 0;
        return compareIds(left.nodeId, right.nodeId) || sizeDimensionOrder[left.dimension] - sizeDimensionOrder[right.dimension];
    }
  }
  var graphIssueOrder = Object.freeze({
    MISSING_EDGE_ENDPOINT: 0,
    MISSING_PARENT: 1,
    PARENT_CYCLE: 2,
    INVALID_POSITION: 3,
    INVALID_SIZE: 4
  });
  var sizeDimensionOrder = Object.freeze({ width: 0, height: 1 });

  // packages/kernel/dist/revision.js
  function incrementRevision(revision) {
    return revision === Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER ? null : revision + 1;
  }

  // packages/kernel/dist/canvas-kernel.js
  function createCanvasKernel() {
    let nodes = new Map;
    let edges = new Map;
    let view = createCanvasView(0, nodes, edges);
    let transactionActive = false;
    return Object.freeze({
      read: () => view,
      transact(callback, metadata) {
        if (transactionActive) {
          throw new KernelError("TRANSACTION_REENTRANT", "A Transaction is already active for this Kernel.", Object.freeze({}));
        }
        const before = view;
        const draftNodes = new Map(nodes);
        const draftEdges = new Map(edges);
        const draftIndex = new GraphIndex(draftNodes, draftEdges);
        const touchedNodeIds = new Set;
        const touchedEdgeIds = new Set;
        let transactionOpen = true;
        const assertTransactionOpen = () => {
          if (!transactionOpen) {
            throw new KernelError("TRANSACTION_CLOSED", "Transaction Context is closed.", Object.freeze({}));
          }
        };
        const query = createCanvasQuery(draftNodes, draftEdges, draftIndex, assertTransactionOpen);
        const nodeWriter = createEntityWriter({
          kind: "node",
          draft: draftNodes,
          touchedIds: touchedNodeIds,
          freeze: freezeCanvasNode,
          assertOpen: assertTransactionOpen,
          onAdd: (node) => draftIndex.addNode(node),
          onReplace: (oldNode, newNode) => draftIndex.replaceNode(oldNode, newNode),
          onRemove: (node) => draftIndex.removeNode(node)
        });
        const edgeWriter = createEntityWriter({
          kind: "edge",
          draft: draftEdges,
          touchedIds: touchedEdgeIds,
          freeze: freezeCanvasEdge,
          assertOpen: assertTransactionOpen,
          onAdd: (edge) => draftIndex.addEdge(edge),
          onReplace: (oldEdge, newEdge) => draftIndex.replaceEdge(oldEdge, newEdge),
          onRemove: (edge) => draftIndex.removeEdge(edge)
        });
        const transaction = Object.freeze({
          query,
          nodes: nodeWriter,
          edges: edgeWriter,
          applyChangeSet(changeSet, direction) {
            assertTransactionOpen();
            applyChangeSetToDraft(changeSet, direction, draftNodes, draftEdges, nodeWriter, edgeWriter);
          }
        });
        transactionActive = true;
        try {
          let callbackResult;
          try {
            callbackResult = callback(transaction);
          } finally {
            transactionOpen = false;
          }
          if (isThenable(callbackResult)) {
            throw new KernelError("ASYNC_TRANSACTION", "Transaction callback must complete synchronously.", Object.freeze({}));
          }
          const issues = validateGraph(draftNodes, draftEdges);
          if (issues.length > 0) {
            throw new KernelError("INVALID_GRAPH", "The final graph is invalid.", Object.freeze({ issues: describeGraphIssues(issues) }));
          }
          const nodeChanges = collectEntityChanges("node", nodes, draftNodes, touchedNodeIds, canvasNodesEqual);
          const edgeChanges = collectEntityChanges("edge", edges, draftEdges, touchedEdgeIds, canvasEdgesEqual);
          const changes = Object.freeze([...nodeChanges, ...edgeChanges]);
          if (changes.length === 0)
            return null;
          const revision = incrementRevision(before.snapshot.revision);
          if (revision === null) {
            throw new KernelError("REVISION_OVERFLOW", "Kernel revision cannot exceed Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER.", Object.freeze({ revision: before.snapshot.revision }));
          }
          const after = createCanvasView(revision, draftNodes, draftEdges);
          const origin = metadata?.origin;
          const commandId = metadata?.commandId;
          const changeSet = Object.freeze({
            beforeRevision: before.snapshot.revision,
            revision,
            ...origin === undefined ? {} : { origin },
            ...commandId === undefined ? {} : { commandId },
            changes
          });
          const commit = Object.freeze({ before, after, changeSet });
          nodes = draftNodes;
          edges = draftEdges;
          view = after;
          return commit;
        } finally {
          transactionActive = false;
        }
      }
    });
  }
  function isThenable(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "object" && typeof value !== "function" || value === null)
      return false;
    return typeof Reflect.get(value, "then") === "function";
  }
  // packages/renderer-api/dist/renderer-error.js
  class RendererError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "RendererError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("renderer", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }
  // packages/renderer-svg/src/svg-renderer-error.ts
  class SvgRendererError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "SvgRendererError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("renderer.svg", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/target-mapping.ts
  function readTargetMatrix(target, code) {
    const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const matrix = target.getScreenCTM();
    const determinant = matrix ? matrix.a * matrix.d - matrix.b * matrix.c : Number.NaN;
    if (!target.isConnected || !Number.isFinite(bounds.width) || !Number.isFinite(bounds.height) || bounds.width <= 0 || bounds.height <= 0 || !matrix || !Number.isFinite(determinant) || determinant === 0) {
      throw new SvgRendererError(code, "SVG Renderer Target must be connected, measurable, and invertible.");
    }
    const inverse = {
      a: matrix.d / determinant,
      b: -matrix.b / determinant,
      c: -matrix.c / determinant,
      d: matrix.a / determinant,
      e: (matrix.c * matrix.f - matrix.d * matrix.e) / determinant,
      f: (matrix.b * matrix.e - matrix.a * matrix.f) / determinant
    };
    const localToUser = {
      a: inverse.a,
      b: inverse.b,
      c: inverse.c,
      d: inverse.d,
      e: inverse.a * bounds.left + inverse.c * bounds.top + inverse.e,
      f: inverse.b * bounds.left + inverse.d * bounds.top + inverse.f
    };
    if (Object.values(localToUser).every(Number.isFinite))
      return localToUser;
    throw new SvgRendererError(code, "SVG Renderer Target coordinate mapping must be finite.");
  }
  function formatSvgMatrix(matrix) {
    return `matrix(${[matrix.a, matrix.b, matrix.c, matrix.d, matrix.e, matrix.f].map(formatMatrixNumber).join(" ")})`;
  }
  function formatMatrixNumber(value) {
    const normalized = Math.abs(value) < 0.000000000001 ? 0 : Math.round(value * 1000000000000) / 1000000000000;
    return String(normalized);
  }

  // packages/port-api/dist/port-query.js
  function getNodePortSnapshot(snapshot, nodeId) {
    return snapshot.nodes.find((candidate) => candidate.nodeId === nodeId);
  }
  function getPortDefinition(snapshot, nodeId, portId) {
    return getNodePortSnapshot(snapshot, nodeId)?.ports.find((candidate) => candidate.id === portId);
  }
  function hasPortDefinitions(snapshot, nodeId) {
    return (getNodePortSnapshot(snapshot, nodeId)?.ports.length ?? 0) > 0;
  }
  function isConnectionTargetDefined(snapshot, target) {
    if (target.portId === undefined)
      return !hasPortDefinitions(snapshot, target.nodeId);
    return getPortDefinition(snapshot, target.nodeId, target.portId)?.role === target.role;
  }
  // packages/renderer-svg/src/port-geometry.ts
  function getNodePortGeometries(node, ports) {
    const snapshot = getNodePortSnapshot(ports, node.id);
    if (!snapshot || !node.size || node.size.width <= 0 || node.size.height <= 0)
      return [];
    return snapshot.ports.map((definition) => {
      const rolePorts = snapshot.ports.filter((candidate) => candidate.role === definition.role);
      const index = rolePorts.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === definition.id);
      return Object.freeze({
        nodeId: node.id,
        portId: definition.id,
        role: definition.role,
        worldPoint: Object.freeze({
          x: definition.role === "source" ? node.position.x + node.size.width : node.position.x,
          y: node.position.y + node.size.height * (index + 1) / (rolePorts.length + 1)
        })
      });
    });
  }
  function resolveConnectionAnchorPoint(document2, ports, anchor) {
    const node = document2.nodes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === anchor.nodeId);
    if (!node?.size || node.size.width <= 0 || node.size.height <= 0) {
      throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Connection Anchor Node is unavailable.", {
        issue: "CONNECTION_ANCHOR_UNAVAILABLE"
      });
    }
    if (anchor.portId === undefined) {
      if (hasPortDefinitions(ports, anchor.nodeId)) {
        throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Node-level Connection Anchor is unavailable.", {
          issue: "NODE_ANCHOR_REPLACED_BY_PORTS"
        });
      }
      return Object.freeze({
        x: anchor.role === "source" ? node.position.x + node.size.width : node.position.x,
        y: node.position.y + node.size.height / 2
      });
    }
    const definition = getPortDefinition(ports, anchor.nodeId, anchor.portId);
    if (!definition || definition.role !== anchor.role) {
      throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Connection Port is unavailable.", {
        issue: "CONNECTION_PORT_UNAVAILABLE"
      });
    }
    const geometry = getNodePortGeometries(node, ports).find((candidate) => candidate.portId === anchor.portId);
    if (!geometry)
      throw new Error("Accepted Port Definition must have SVG Geometry.");
    return geometry.worldPoint;
  }
  function resolveEdgeEndpointPoint(nodesById, ports, endpoint, role, edgeId) {
    const node = nodesById.get(endpoint.nodeId);
    if (!node?.size) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Edge endpoints require sized Nodes.", {
        issue: "MISSING_NODE_SIZE",
        edgeId
      });
    }
    if (endpoint.portId === undefined) {
      return Object.freeze({
        x: node.position.x + node.size.width / 2,
        y: node.position.y + node.size.height / 2
      });
    }
    const definition = getPortDefinition(ports, endpoint.nodeId, endpoint.portId);
    if (!definition || definition.role !== role) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Edge Port Geometry is unavailable.", {
        issue: "UNAVAILABLE_PORT_GEOMETRY",
        edgeId,
        endpoint: role,
        portId: endpoint.portId
      });
    }
    const geometry = getNodePortGeometries(node, ports).find((candidate) => candidate.portId === endpoint.portId);
    if (!geometry)
      throw new Error("Accepted Port Definition must have SVG Geometry.");
    return geometry.worldPoint;
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/hit-test.ts
  function hitTestProjection(point, target, documentSnapshot, portSnapshot, sessionSnapshot, edgeHitTolerance, connectionAnchorHitTolerance) {
    validateScreenPoint(point);
    readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
    const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (point.x < 0 || point.y < 0 || point.x >= bounds.width || point.y >= bounds.height)
      return null;
    const viewport = sessionSnapshot.viewport;
    const worldPoint = {
      x: (point.x - viewport.x) / viewport.zoom,
      y: (point.y - viewport.y) / viewport.zoom
    };
    for (let index = documentSnapshot.nodes.length - 1;index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const node = documentSnapshot.nodes[index];
      if (!node?.size || node.size.width <= 0 || node.size.height <= 0)
        continue;
      const portGeometries = getNodePortGeometries(node, portSnapshot);
      for (let portIndex = portGeometries.length - 1;portIndex >= 0; portIndex -= 1) {
        const geometry = portGeometries[portIndex];
        if (!geometry)
          continue;
        const portScreenPoint = {
          x: geometry.worldPoint.x * viewport.zoom + viewport.x,
          y: geometry.worldPoint.y * viewport.zoom + viewport.y
        };
        if (Math.hypot(point.x - portScreenPoint.x, point.y - portScreenPoint.y) <= connectionAnchorHitTolerance) {
          return {
            type: "port",
            nodeId: geometry.nodeId,
            portId: geometry.portId,
            role: geometry.role,
            worldPoint
          };
        }
      }
      if (portGeometries.length > 0)
        continue;
      for (const role of ["source", "target"]) {
        const anchorWorldPoint = {
          x: role === "source" ? node.position.x + node.size.width : node.position.x,
          y: node.position.y + node.size.height / 2
        };
        const anchorScreenPoint = {
          x: anchorWorldPoint.x * viewport.zoom + viewport.x,
          y: anchorWorldPoint.y * viewport.zoom + viewport.y
        };
        if (Math.hypot(point.x - anchorScreenPoint.x, point.y - anchorScreenPoint.y) <= connectionAnchorHitTolerance) {
          return { type: "connection-anchor", nodeId: node.id, role, worldPoint };
        }
      }
    }
    for (let index = documentSnapshot.nodes.length - 1;index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const node = documentSnapshot.nodes[index];
      if (!node?.size)
        continue;
      if (worldPoint.x >= node.position.x && worldPoint.x <= node.position.x + node.size.width && worldPoint.y >= node.position.y && worldPoint.y <= node.position.y + node.size.height) {
        return { type: "node", nodeId: node.id, worldPoint };
      }
    }
    const nodesById = new Map(documentSnapshot.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    for (let index = documentSnapshot.edges.length - 1;index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const edge = documentSnapshot.edges[index];
      if (!edge)
        continue;
      const source = resolveEdgeEndpointPoint(nodesById, portSnapshot, edge.source, "source", edge.id);
      const destination = resolveEdgeEndpointPoint(nodesById, portSnapshot, edge.target, "target", edge.id);
      const sourceScreen = {
        x: source.x * viewport.zoom + viewport.x,
        y: source.y * viewport.zoom + viewport.y
      };
      const destinationScreen = {
        x: destination.x * viewport.zoom + viewport.x,
        y: destination.y * viewport.zoom + viewport.y
      };
      if (distanceToSegment(point, sourceScreen, destinationScreen) <= edgeHitTolerance) {
        return { type: "edge", edgeId: edge.id, worldPoint };
      }
    }
    return { type: "canvas", worldPoint };
  }
  function validateScreenPoint(point) {
    if (!isRecord2(point) || typeof point.x !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(point.x)) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_SCREEN_POINT", "SVG Renderer Screen Point x must be finite.", { field: "x" });
    }
    if (typeof point.y !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(point.y)) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_SCREEN_POINT", "SVG Renderer Screen Point y must be finite.", { field: "y" });
    }
  }
  function distanceToSegment(point, start, end) {
    const dx = end.x - start.x;
    const dy = end.y - start.y;
    const squaredLength = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (squaredLength === 0)
      return Math.hypot(point.x - start.x, point.y - start.y);
    const projection = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((point.x - start.x) * dx + (point.y - start.y) * dy) / squaredLength));
    return Math.hypot(point.x - (start.x + projection * dx), point.y - (start.y + projection * dy));
  }
  function isRecord2(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/input-normalization.ts
  function applyDomEventPolicy(event, policy) {
    if (policy.preventDefault)
      event.preventDefault();
    if (policy.stopPropagation)
      event.stopPropagation();
  }
  function normalizePointerInput(event, target, session, type = normalizePointerInputType(event.type)) {
    const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const screenPoint = {
      x: event.clientX - bounds.left,
      y: event.clientY - bounds.top
    };
    const viewport = session.viewport;
    return Object.freeze({
      type,
      pointerId: event.pointerId,
      pointerType: normalizePointerType(event.pointerType),
      screenPoint: Object.freeze(screenPoint),
      worldPoint: Object.freeze({
        x: (screenPoint.x - viewport.x) / viewport.zoom,
        y: (screenPoint.y - viewport.y) / viewport.zoom
      }),
      button: type === "pointer.move" || type === "pointer.cancel" ? null : normalizePointerButton(event.button),
      pressedButtons: Object.freeze(normalizePressedButtons(event.buttons)),
      modifiers: normalizeInputModifiers(event)
    });
  }
  function normalizeWheelInput(event, target, session) {
    const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const screenPoint = {
      x: event.clientX - bounds.left,
      y: event.clientY - bounds.top
    };
    const viewport = session.viewport;
    const factor = event.deltaMode === WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_LINE ? 16 : event.deltaMode === WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_PAGE ? bounds.height : 1;
    return Object.freeze({
      type: "wheel",
      screenPoint: Object.freeze(screenPoint),
      worldPoint: Object.freeze({
        x: (screenPoint.x - viewport.x) / viewport.zoom,
        y: (screenPoint.y - viewport.y) / viewport.zoom
      }),
      deltaX: event.deltaX * factor,
      deltaY: event.deltaY * factor,
      modifiers: normalizeInputModifiers(event)
    });
  }
  function normalizeKeyboardInput(event) {
    return Object.freeze({
      type: event.type === "keydown" ? "key.down" : "key.up",
      key: event.key,
      code: event.code,
      repeat: event.repeat,
      modifiers: normalizeInputModifiers(event)
    });
  }
  function normalizePointerInputType(type) {
    switch (type) {
      case "pointerdown":
        return "pointer.down";
      case "pointermove":
        return "pointer.move";
      case "pointerup":
        return "pointer.up";
      case "pointercancel":
        return "pointer.cancel";
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unsupported Pointer event type: ${type}`);
    }
  }
  function normalizePointerType(type) {
    return type === "mouse" || type === "pen" || type === "touch" ? type : "unknown";
  }
  function normalizePointerButton(button) {
    return {
      0: "primary",
      1: "auxiliary",
      2: "secondary",
      3: "back",
      4: "forward"
    }[button] ?? null;
  }
  function normalizePressedButtons(buttons) {
    const pressed = [];
    if ((buttons & 1) !== 0)
      pressed.push("primary");
    if ((buttons & 4) !== 0)
      pressed.push("auxiliary");
    if ((buttons & 2) !== 0)
      pressed.push("secondary");
    if ((buttons & 8) !== 0)
      pressed.push("back");
    if ((buttons & 16) !== 0)
      pressed.push("forward");
    return pressed;
  }
  function normalizeInputModifiers(event) {
    return Object.freeze({
      alt: event.altKey,
      control: event.ctrlKey,
      meta: event.metaKey,
      shift: event.shiftKey
    });
  }
  function assertActivePointer(pointerId, activePointerIds) {
    if (Number.isSafeInteger(pointerId) && pointerId >= 0 && activePointerIds.has(pointerId))
      return;
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_POINTER", "SVG Renderer Pointer must be active.", { pointerId });
  }
  function releaseNativePointerCapture(target, pointerId, capturedPointerIds) {
    if (!capturedPointerIds.has(pointerId))
      return;
    capturedPointerIds.delete(pointerId);
    try {
      if (target.hasPointerCapture(pointerId))
        target.releasePointerCapture(pointerId);
    } catch (error) {
      capturedPointerIds.add(pointerId);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/renderer-state.ts
  function cloneCanvasSnapshot(snapshot) {
    return {
      revision: snapshot.revision,
      nodes: snapshot.nodes.map((node) => ({
        id: node.id,
        type: node.type,
        position: { x: node.position.x, y: node.position.y },
        ...node.size === undefined ? {} : { size: { width: node.size.width, height: node.size.height } },
        ...node.parentId === undefined ? {} : { parentId: node.parentId },
        data: node.data
      })),
      edges: snapshot.edges.map((edge) => ({
        id: edge.id,
        type: edge.type,
        source: {
          nodeId: edge.source.nodeId,
          ...edge.source.portId === undefined ? {} : { portId: edge.source.portId }
        },
        target: {
          nodeId: edge.target.nodeId,
          ...edge.target.portId === undefined ? {} : { portId: edge.target.portId }
        },
        data: edge.data
      }))
    };
  }
  function validateRendererDocumentUpdate(value) {
    if (!isRecord3(value) || value.type !== "reset" && value.type !== "commit") {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Document Update is invalid.", {
        issue: "INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE"
      });
    }
    if (value.type === "reset") {
      validateCanvasView(value.view);
      return value;
    }
    validateCanvasCommit(value.commit);
    return value;
  }
  function acceptPortSnapshot(value, document2) {
    if (value === undefined) {
      return Object.freeze({
        documentRevision: document2.revision,
        generation: 0,
        nodes: Object.freeze([])
      });
    }
    if (!isRecord3(value) || value.documentRevision !== document2.revision || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.generation) || value.generation < 0 || !Array.isArray(value.nodes)) {
      throwInvalidPortSnapshot("PORT_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH");
    }
    const documentNodeIds = new Set(document2.nodes.map((node) => node.id));
    let previousNodeId;
    const nodes = value.nodes.map((candidate) => {
      if (!isRecord3(candidate) || typeof candidate.nodeId !== "string" || candidate.nodeId.length === 0 || !documentNodeIds.has(candidate.nodeId) || !Array.isArray(candidate.ports) || candidate.ports.length === 0 || previousNodeId !== undefined && previousNodeId >= candidate.nodeId) {
        throwInvalidPortSnapshot("INVALID_NODE_PORTS");
      }
      previousNodeId = candidate.nodeId;
      let previousPortId;
      const ports = candidate.ports.map((port) => {
        if (!isRecord3(port) || typeof port.id !== "string" || port.id.length === 0 || port.role !== "source" && port.role !== "target" || previousPortId !== undefined && previousPortId >= port.id) {
          throwInvalidPortSnapshot("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION");
        }
        previousPortId = port.id;
        return Object.freeze({ id: port.id, role: port.role });
      });
      return Object.freeze({ nodeId: candidate.nodeId, ports: Object.freeze(ports) });
    });
    return Object.freeze({
      documentRevision: value.documentRevision,
      generation: value.generation,
      nodes: Object.freeze(nodes)
    });
  }
  function throwInvalidPortSnapshot(issue) {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Port Snapshot is invalid.", { issue });
  }
  function validateCanvasCommit(value) {
    if (!isRecord3(value))
      throwInvalidCommit();
    validateCanvasView(value.before);
    validateCanvasView(value.after);
    if (!isRecord3(value.changeSet))
      throwInvalidCommit();
    const changeSet = value.changeSet;
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(changeSet.beforeRevision) || !Number.isSafeInteger(changeSet.revision) || !Array.isArray(changeSet.changes)) {
      throwInvalidCommit();
    }
    for (const change of changeSet.changes) {
      if (!isRecord3(change) || change.entity !== "node" && change.entity !== "edge" || typeof change.id !== "string" || change.id.length === 0 || !("before" in change) || !("after" in change) || change.before === null && change.after === null || !isChangeEntityValue(change.entity, change.id, change.before) || !isChangeEntityValue(change.entity, change.id, change.after)) {
        throwInvalidCommit();
      }
    }
  }
  function isChangeEntityValue(entity, id, value) {
    if (value === null)
      return true;
    if (entity === "node")
      return isCanvasNodeShell(value) && value.id === id;
    return isCanvasEdgeShell(value) && value.id === id;
  }
  function throwInvalidCommit() {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Canvas Commit is invalid.", {
      issue: "INVALID_DOCUMENT_COMMIT"
    });
  }
  function validateCanvasView(value) {
    if (!isRecord3(value) || !isRecord3(value.snapshot))
      throwInvalidSnapshot("view", "INVALID_VIEW");
    const snapshot = value.snapshot;
    if (typeof snapshot.revision !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(snapshot.revision) || snapshot.revision < 0) {
      throwInvalidSnapshot("revision", "INVALID_REVISION");
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.nodes))
      throwInvalidSnapshot("nodes", "INVALID_ARRAY");
    if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.edges))
      throwInvalidSnapshot("edges", "INVALID_ARRAY");
    if (!isCanvasQuerySurface(value.query))
      throwInvalidSnapshot("query", "INVALID_QUERY");
    validateCanonicalIds(snapshot.nodes, "nodes");
    validateCanonicalIds(snapshot.edges, "edges");
    const nodeIds = new Set;
    const nodes = [];
    for (const node of snapshot.nodes) {
      if (!isCanvasNodeShell(node))
        throwInvalidSnapshot("nodes", "INVALID_ENTITY");
      nodeIds.add(node.id);
      nodes.push(node);
    }
    for (const node of nodes) {
      if (node.parentId !== undefined && !nodeIds.has(node.parentId)) {
        throwInvalidSnapshot("nodes", "MISSING_PARENT");
      }
    }
    validateParentAcyclic(nodes);
    for (const edge of snapshot.edges) {
      if (!isCanvasEdgeShell(edge))
        throwInvalidSnapshot("edges", "INVALID_ENTITY");
      if (!nodeIds.has(edge.source.nodeId) || !nodeIds.has(edge.target.nodeId)) {
        throwInvalidSnapshot("edges", "MISSING_EDGE_ENDPOINT");
      }
    }
  }
  function isCanvasQuerySurface(value) {
    if (!isRecord3(value))
      return false;
    return ["getNode", "getEdge", "getIncomingEdges", "getOutgoingEdges", "getIncidentEdges", "getChildren"].every((method) => typeof value[method] === "function");
  }
  function validateParentAcyclic(nodes) {
    const nodesById = new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    const states = new Map;
    const visit = (id) => {
      const state = states.get(id);
      if (state === "done")
        return;
      if (state === "visiting")
        throwInvalidSnapshot("nodes", "PARENT_CYCLE");
      states.set(id, "visiting");
      const parentId = nodesById.get(id)?.parentId;
      if (parentId !== undefined)
        visit(parentId);
      states.set(id, "done");
    };
    for (const node of nodes)
      visit(node.id);
  }
  function validateCanonicalIds(entities, field) {
    let previous;
    for (const entity of entities) {
      if (!isRecord3(entity) || typeof entity.id !== "string" || entity.id.length === 0) {
        throwInvalidSnapshot(field, "INVALID_ID");
      }
      if (previous !== undefined && previous >= entity.id)
        throwInvalidSnapshot(field, "NON_CANONICAL_IDS");
      previous = entity.id;
    }
  }
  function isCanvasNodeShell(value) {
    if (!isRecord3(value) || typeof value.id !== "string" || typeof value.type !== "string" || !Object.hasOwn(value, "data")) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!isPoint(value.position))
      return false;
    if (value.size !== undefined && !isSize(value.size))
      return false;
    return value.parentId === undefined || typeof value.parentId === "string" && value.parentId.length > 0;
  }
  function isCanvasEdgeShell(value) {
    return isRecord3(value) && typeof value.id === "string" && typeof value.type === "string" && Object.hasOwn(value, "data") && isEndpoint(value.source) && isEndpoint(value.target);
  }
  function isPoint(value) {
    return isRecord3(value) && typeof value.x === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.x) && typeof value.y === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.y);
  }
  function isSize(value) {
    return isRecord3(value) && typeof value.width === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.width) && value.width > 0 && typeof value.height === "number" && Number.isFinite(value.height) && value.height > 0;
  }
  function isEndpoint(value) {
    return isRecord3(value) && typeof value.nodeId === "string" && value.nodeId.length > 0 && (value.portId === undefined || typeof value.portId === "string");
  }
  function isRecord3(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }
  function throwInvalidSnapshot(field, reason) {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Document Snapshot is invalid.", {
      issue: "INVALID_DOCUMENT_SNAPSHOT",
      field,
      reason
    });
  }
  function validateSessionSnapshot(snapshot, documentSnapshot) {
    if (!isRecord3(snapshot) || !isRecord3(snapshot.selection) || !isRecord3(snapshot.viewport)) {
      throwInvalidSession("INVALID_SESSION_STRUCTURE");
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.selection.nodeIds))
      throwInvalidSession("INVALID_SELECTION", { field: "nodeIds" });
    if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.selection.edgeIds))
      throwInvalidSession("INVALID_SELECTION", { field: "edgeIds" });
    const nodeIds = new Set(documentSnapshot.nodes.map((node) => node.id));
    const edgeIds = new Set(documentSnapshot.edges.map((edge) => edge.id));
    validateSelectionIds(snapshot.selection.nodeIds, "nodeIds", "node", nodeIds);
    validateSelectionIds(snapshot.selection.edgeIds, "edgeIds", "edge", edgeIds);
    for (const field of ["x", "y", "zoom"]) {
      const value = snapshot.viewport[field];
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || field === "zoom" && value <= 0) {
        throwInvalidSession("INVALID_VIEWPORT", { field });
      }
    }
  }
  function validateSelectionIds(ids, field, entity, availableIds) {
    let previous;
    for (const id of ids) {
      if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
        throwInvalidSession("INVALID_SELECTION", { field });
      if (previous === id)
        throwInvalidSession("DUPLICATE_SELECTION_ID", { field, id });
      if (previous !== undefined && previous > id)
        throwInvalidSession("NON_CANONICAL_SELECTION", { field });
      if (!availableIds.has(id))
        throwInvalidSession("SELECTION_ENTITY_MISSING", { entity, id });
      previous = id;
    }
  }
  function throwInvalidSession(issue, details = {}) {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Session Snapshot is invalid.", {
      issue,
      ...details
    });
  }
  function cloneSessionSnapshot(snapshot) {
    return {
      selection: {
        nodeIds: [...snapshot.selection.nodeIds],
        edgeIds: [...snapshot.selection.edgeIds]
      },
      viewport: {
        x: snapshot.viewport.x,
        y: snapshot.viewport.y,
        zoom: snapshot.viewport.zoom
      }
    };
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/svg-config.ts
  function validateConfig(config) {
    if (typeof config !== "object" || config === null) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "SVG Renderer config must be an object.");
    }
    assertKnownConfigKeys(config, ["target", "edgeHitTolerance", "connectionAnchorHitTolerance", "input"], "");
    const target = config.target;
    const ownerDocument = isRecord4(target) ? Reflect.get(target, "ownerDocument") : undefined;
    const ownerWindow = isRecord4(ownerDocument) ? Reflect.get(ownerDocument, "defaultView") : undefined;
    if (!isRecord4(ownerWindow) || typeof ownerWindow.SVGSVGElement !== "function" || !(target instanceof ownerWindow.SVGSVGElement)) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_TARGET", "SVG Renderer Target must be an SVGSVGElement.");
    }
    const edgeHitTolerance = config.edgeHitTolerance;
    if (edgeHitTolerance !== undefined && (!Number.isFinite(edgeHitTolerance) || edgeHitTolerance < 0)) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "edgeHitTolerance must be a finite non-negative number.", {
        field: "edgeHitTolerance"
      });
    }
    const connectionAnchorHitTolerance = config.connectionAnchorHitTolerance;
    if (connectionAnchorHitTolerance !== undefined && (!Number.isFinite(connectionAnchorHitTolerance) || connectionAnchorHitTolerance < 0)) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "connectionAnchorHitTolerance must be a finite non-negative number.", {
        field: "connectionAnchorHitTolerance"
      });
    }
    return {
      target,
      edgeHitTolerance: edgeHitTolerance ?? 4,
      connectionAnchorHitTolerance: connectionAnchorHitTolerance ?? 8
    };
  }
  function normalizeInputPolicies(input) {
    if (input !== undefined && !isRecord4(input)) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "SVG Renderer input policy must be an object.", { field: "input" });
    }
    if (input !== undefined) {
      assertKnownConfigKeys(input, ["pointer", "wheel", "keyboard", "contextMenu"], "input");
    }
    const policies = input;
    return {
      pointer: normalizeDomEventPolicy(policies?.pointer, "pointer"),
      wheel: normalizeDomEventPolicy(policies?.wheel, "wheel"),
      keyboard: normalizeDomEventPolicy(policies?.keyboard, "keyboard"),
      contextMenu: normalizeDomEventPolicy(policies?.contextMenu, "contextMenu")
    };
  }
  function normalizeDomEventPolicy(policy, field) {
    if (policy !== undefined && !isRecord4(policy)) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "SVG Renderer DOM event policy must be an object.", { field });
    }
    if (policy !== undefined)
      assertKnownConfigKeys(policy, ["preventDefault", "stopPropagation"], `input.${field}`);
    const normalized = policy;
    const accepted = { preventDefault: false, stopPropagation: false };
    for (const key of ["preventDefault", "stopPropagation"]) {
      const value = normalized?.[key];
      if (value !== undefined && typeof value !== "boolean") {
        throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "SVG Renderer DOM event policy values must be boolean.", {
          field: `${field}.${key}`
        });
      }
      accepted[key] = value ?? false;
    }
    return accepted;
  }
  function assertKnownConfigKeys(value, allowedKeys, prefix) {
    const allowed = new Set(allowedKeys);
    for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
      if (typeof key === "string" && allowed.has(key))
        continue;
      const field = typeof key === "string" ? prefix.length === 0 ? key : `${prefix}.${key}` : `${prefix || "config"}.[symbol]`;
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_CONFIG", "SVG Renderer config contains an unknown field.", { field });
    }
  }
  function isRecord4(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/svg-projection.ts
  var SVG_NAMESPACE = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  function renderReset(view, document2, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer, ports) {
    validateCanvasView(view);
    const nodes = view.snapshot.nodes.map((node) => createNodeElement(document2, node));
    const nodesById = new Map(view.snapshot.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    const edges = view.snapshot.edges.map((edge) => createEdgeElement(document2, edge, nodesById, ports));
    edgesLayer.replaceChildren(...edges);
    nodesLayer.replaceChildren(...nodes);
    interactionLayer.replaceChildren(...view.snapshot.nodes.flatMap((node) => createConnectionTargetElements(document2, node, ports)));
  }
  function applyCommit(commit, baselineSnapshot, acceptedSession, document2, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer, ports, completeProjection) {
    validateCanvasView(commit.before);
    validateCanvasView(commit.after);
    const baselineRevision = baselineSnapshot?.revision;
    const beforeRevision = commit.before.snapshot.revision;
    const afterRevision = commit.after.snapshot.revision;
    if (baselineRevision === undefined || beforeRevision !== baselineRevision || afterRevision !== beforeRevision + 1 || commit.changeSet.beforeRevision !== beforeRevision || commit.changeSet.revision !== afterRevision) {
      throw new RendererError("DOCUMENT_OUT_OF_SYNC", "SVG Renderer Commit is not contiguous with its Baseline.", {
        expectedRevision: baselineRevision ?? null,
        receivedRevision: beforeRevision
      });
    }
    if (!canvasSnapshotsEqual(baselineSnapshot, commit.before.snapshot)) {
      throw new RendererError("DOCUMENT_OUT_OF_SYNC", "SVG Renderer Commit before View differs from its Baseline.", {
        issue: "BASELINE_CONTENT_MISMATCH",
        expectedRevision: baselineRevision,
        receivedRevision: beforeRevision
      });
    }
    validateChangeSetConsistency(commit);
    if (acceptedSession)
      validateSessionSnapshot(acceptedSession, commit.after.snapshot);
    const afterNodesById = new Map(commit.after.snapshot.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    const validatedNodes = new Map(commit.after.snapshot.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, createNodeElement(document2, node)]));
    const validatedEdges = new Map(commit.after.snapshot.edges.map((edge) => [edge.id, createEdgeElement(document2, edge, afterNodesById, ports)]));
    const changedNodeIds = new Set(commit.changeSet.changes.filter((change) => change.entity === "node").map((change) => change.id));
    const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer);
    try {
      for (const change of commit.changeSet.changes) {
        if (change.entity === "node")
          applyNodeChange(change, validatedNodes, nodesLayer, journal);
        else
          applyEdgeChange(change, validatedEdges, edgesLayer, journal);
      }
      for (const edge of commit.after.snapshot.edges) {
        if (!changedNodeIds.has(edge.source.nodeId) && !changedNodeIds.has(edge.target.nodeId))
          continue;
        const existing = requireEntityElement(edgesLayer, "data-nodebraid-edge-id", edge.id);
        const validated = requireMapValue(validatedEdges, edge.id);
        copyGeometryAttributes(validated, existing, ["x1", "y1", "x2", "y2"], journal);
      }
      nodesLayer.append(...commit.after.snapshot.nodes.map((node) => requireEntityElement(nodesLayer, "data-nodebraid-node-id", node.id)));
      edgesLayer.append(...commit.after.snapshot.edges.map((edge) => requireEntityElement(edgesLayer, "data-nodebraid-edge-id", edge.id)));
      syncConnectionTargets(commit.after.snapshot.nodes, document2, interactionLayer, ports, journal);
      completeProjection?.(journal);
    } catch (error) {
      const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
      if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
        throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }
  function syncConnectionTargets(nodes, document2, interactionLayer, ports, journal) {
    const ordered = [];
    const existingTargets = Array.from(interactionLayer.querySelectorAll("[data-nodebraid-connection-anchor-node-id], [data-nodebraid-port-node-id]"));
    const existingTargetsByKey = new Map(existingTargets.map((element) => [connectionTargetKey(element), element]));
    for (const node of nodes) {
      for (const candidate of createConnectionTargetElements(document2, node, ports)) {
        const key = connectionTargetKey(candidate);
        const existing = existingTargetsByKey.get(key);
        if (existing) {
          copyGeometryAttributes(candidate, existing, ["cx", "cy"], journal);
          ordered.push(existing);
          existingTargetsByKey.delete(key);
        } else {
          ordered.push(candidate);
        }
      }
    }
    for (const element of existingTargetsByKey.values())
      element.remove();
    interactionLayer.append(...ordered, ...interactionLayer.querySelectorAll("[data-nodebraid-connection-preview]"));
  }
  function createConnectionTargetElements(document2, node, ports) {
    const size = node.size;
    if (!size || size.width <= 0 || size.height <= 0)
      return [];
    const portGeometries = getNodePortGeometries(node, ports);
    if (portGeometries.length > 0) {
      return portGeometries.map((geometry) => {
        const element = createSvgElement(document2, "circle");
        element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__port");
        element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-node-id", node.id);
        element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-id", geometry.portId);
        element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-role", geometry.role);
        element.setAttribute("cx", String(geometry.worldPoint.x));
        element.setAttribute("cy", String(geometry.worldPoint.y));
        return element;
      });
    }
    return ["target", "source"].map((role) => {
      const element = createSvgElement(document2, "circle");
      element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__connection-anchor");
      element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-connection-anchor-node-id", node.id);
      element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-connection-anchor-role", role);
      element.setAttribute("cx", String(role === "source" ? node.position.x + size.width : node.position.x));
      element.setAttribute("cy", String(node.position.y + size.height / 2));
      return element;
    });
  }
  function connectionTargetKey(element) {
    const portNodeId = element.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-node-id");
    if (portNodeId !== null) {
      return `port\x00${portNodeId}\x00${element.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-id")}\x00${element.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-port-role")}`;
    }
    return `anchor\x00${element.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-connection-anchor-node-id")}\x00${element.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-connection-anchor-role")}`;
  }
  function applyConnectionPreview(preview, snapshot, ports, interactionLayer, journal) {
    const source = resolveConnectionAnchorPoint(snapshot, ports, preview.source);
    let endpoint = preview.pointerWorldPoint;
    if (preview.target.type !== "none") {
      const targetAnchor = preview.target.anchor;
      endpoint = resolveConnectionAnchorPoint(snapshot, ports, targetAnchor);
    }
    let element = interactionLayer.querySelector("[data-nodebraid-connection-preview]");
    if (!element) {
      element = createSvgElement(interactionLayer.ownerDocument, "line");
      element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__connection-preview");
      element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-connection-preview", "");
      interactionLayer.append(element);
    }
    setElementAttribute(element, "x1", String(source.x), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "y1", String(source.y), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "x2", String(endpoint.x), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "y2", String(endpoint.y), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "data-nodebraid-connection-target", preview.target.type, journal);
  }
  function clearConnectionPreview(interactionLayer) {
    interactionLayer.querySelector("[data-nodebraid-connection-preview]")?.remove();
  }
  function applyBoxSelection(projection, interactionLayer, journal) {
    let element = interactionLayer.querySelector("[data-nodebraid-box-selection]");
    if (!element) {
      element = createSvgElement(interactionLayer.ownerDocument, "rect");
      element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__box-selection");
      element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-box-selection", "");
      interactionLayer.append(element);
    }
    setElementAttribute(element, "x", String(projection.rect.x), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "y", String(projection.rect.y), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "width", String(projection.rect.width), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "height", String(projection.rect.height), journal);
  }
  function clearBoxSelection(interactionLayer) {
    interactionLayer.querySelector("[data-nodebraid-box-selection]")?.remove();
  }
  function applyNodeDragProjection(projection, baselineSnapshot, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, ports, suppliedJournal) {
    const journal = suppliedJournal ?? new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer);
    try {
      const candidates = new Map(projection.nodes.map((candidate) => [candidate.nodeId, candidate]));
      const effectiveNodes = new Map(baselineSnapshot.nodes.map((node) => {
        const candidate = candidates.get(node.id);
        return [node.id, candidate ? { ...node, position: candidate.position } : node];
      }));
      for (const candidate of projection.nodes) {
        const element = requireEntityElement(nodesLayer, "data-nodebraid-node-id", candidate.nodeId);
        setElementAttribute(element, "x", String(candidate.position.x), journal);
        setElementAttribute(element, "y", String(candidate.position.y), journal);
      }
      for (const edge of baselineSnapshot.edges) {
        if (!candidates.has(edge.source.nodeId) && !candidates.has(edge.target.nodeId))
          continue;
        const existing = requireEntityElement(edgesLayer, "data-nodebraid-edge-id", edge.id);
        const validated = createEdgeElement(edgesLayer.ownerDocument, edge, effectiveNodes, ports);
        copyGeometryAttributes(validated, existing, ["x1", "y1", "x2", "y2"], journal);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (suppliedJournal)
        throw error;
      const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
      if (rollbackErrors.length > 0)
        throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  function applyNodeChange(change, validatedNodes, nodesLayer, journal) {
    const existing = findEntityElement(nodesLayer, "data-nodebraid-node-id", change.id);
    if (change.before === null && change.after !== null) {
      if (existing)
        throwBaselineEntityConflict("node", change.id, "missing", "present");
      nodesLayer.append(requireMapValue(validatedNodes, change.id));
      return;
    }
    if (change.before !== null && change.after === null) {
      if (!existing)
        throwBaselineEntityConflict("node", change.id, "present", "missing");
      existing.remove();
      return;
    }
    if (!existing || change.after === null)
      throwBaselineEntityConflict("node", change.id, "present", "missing");
    updateNodeElement(existing, change.after, journal);
  }
  function applyEdgeChange(change, validatedEdges, edgesLayer, journal) {
    const existing = findEntityElement(edgesLayer, "data-nodebraid-edge-id", change.id);
    if (change.before === null && change.after !== null) {
      if (existing)
        throwBaselineEntityConflict("edge", change.id, "missing", "present");
      edgesLayer.append(requireMapValue(validatedEdges, change.id));
      return;
    }
    if (change.before !== null && change.after === null) {
      if (!existing)
        throwBaselineEntityConflict("edge", change.id, "present", "missing");
      existing.remove();
      return;
    }
    if (!existing || change.after === null)
      throwBaselineEntityConflict("edge", change.id, "present", "missing");
    const validated = requireMapValue(validatedEdges, change.id);
    copyGeometryAttributes(validated, existing, ["x1", "y1", "x2", "y2"], journal);
  }
  function createNodeElement(document2, node) {
    if (!node.size) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Node projection requires an explicit Size.", {
        issue: "MISSING_NODE_SIZE",
        nodeId: node.id
      });
    }
    const element = createSvgElement(document2, "rect");
    element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__node");
    element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-node-id", node.id);
    updateNodeElement(element, node);
    return element;
  }
  function updateNodeElement(element, node, journal) {
    if (!node.size) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Node projection requires an explicit Size.", {
        issue: "MISSING_NODE_SIZE",
        nodeId: node.id
      });
    }
    setElementAttribute(element, "x", String(node.position.x), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "y", String(node.position.y), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "width", String(node.size.width), journal);
    setElementAttribute(element, "height", String(node.size.height), journal);
  }
  function createEdgeElement(document2, edge, nodesById, ports) {
    if (edge.source.nodeId === edge.target.nodeId) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer cannot project a self-loop as a straight Edge.", {
        issue: "UNSUPPORTED_SELF_LOOP",
        edgeId: edge.id
      });
    }
    const source = resolveEdgeEndpointPoint(nodesById, ports, edge.source, "source", edge.id);
    const target = resolveEdgeEndpointPoint(nodesById, ports, edge.target, "target", edge.id);
    const element = createSvgElement(document2, "line");
    element.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__edge");
    element.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-edge-id", edge.id);
    element.setAttribute("x1", String(source.x));
    element.setAttribute("y1", String(source.y));
    element.setAttribute("x2", String(target.x));
    element.setAttribute("y2", String(target.y));
    return element;
  }
  function createSvgElement(document2, name) {
    return document2.createElementNS(SVG_NAMESPACE, name);
  }
  function setSelected(element, selected, journal) {
    if (selected)
      setElementAttribute(element, "data-nodebraid-selected", "true", journal);
    else
      removeElementAttribute(element, "data-nodebraid-selected", journal);
  }
  function findEntityElement(layer, attribute, id) {
    return Array.from(layer.children).find((element) => element.getAttribute(attribute) === id);
  }
  function requireEntityElement(layer, attribute, id) {
    const element = findEntityElement(layer, attribute, id);
    if (!element)
      throwBaselineEntityConflict(attribute.includes("node") ? "node" : "edge", id, "present", "missing");
    return element;
  }
  function requireMapValue(map, key) {
    const value = map.get(key);
    if (!value)
      throw new Error("Validated SVG Projection value is missing.");
    return value;
  }
  function copyGeometryAttributes(source, target, attributes, journal) {
    for (const attribute of attributes) {
      const value = source.getAttribute(attribute);
      if (value === null)
        removeElementAttribute(target, attribute, journal);
      else
        setElementAttribute(target, attribute, value, journal);
    }
  }
  function throwBaselineEntityConflict(entity, id, expectedState, actualState) {
    throw new RendererError("DOCUMENT_OUT_OF_SYNC", "SVG Renderer Baseline entity state does not match the Commit.", {
      entity,
      id,
      expectedState,
      actualState
    });
  }

  class DomMutationJournal {
    edgesLayer;
    nodesLayer;
    interactionLayer;
    #edgeChildren;
    #nodeChildren;
    #interactionChildren;
    #attributeUndos = [];
    constructor(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer) {
      this.edgesLayer = edgesLayer;
      this.nodesLayer = nodesLayer;
      this.interactionLayer = interactionLayer;
