      this.#edgeChildren = [...edgesLayer.children];
      this.#nodeChildren = [...nodesLayer.children];
      this.#interactionChildren = interactionLayer ? [...interactionLayer.children] : undefined;
    }
    setAttribute(element, name, value) {
      const previous = element.getAttribute(name);
      if (previous === value)
        return;
      this.#attributeUndos.push(() => {
        if (previous === null)
          element.removeAttribute(name);
        else
          element.setAttribute(name, previous);
      });
      element.setAttribute(name, value);
    }
    removeAttribute(element, name) {
      const previous = element.getAttribute(name);
      if (previous === null)
        return;
      this.#attributeUndos.push(() => element.setAttribute(name, previous));
      element.removeAttribute(name);
    }
    rollback() {
      const errors = [];
      for (const undo of [...this.#attributeUndos].reverse()) {
        try {
          undo();
        } catch (error) {
          errors.push(error);
        }
      }
      const layers = [
        [this.edgesLayer, this.#edgeChildren],
        [this.nodesLayer, this.#nodeChildren],
        ...this.interactionLayer && this.#interactionChildren ? [[this.interactionLayer, this.#interactionChildren]] : []
      ];
      for (const [layer, children] of layers) {
        try {
          layer.replaceChildren(...children);
        } catch (error) {
          errors.push(error);
        }
      }
      return errors;
    }
  }

  class ProjectionRollbackError extends AggregateError {
    constructor(errors) {
      super(errors, "SVG Renderer Projection update and DOM rollback both failed.");
    }
  }
  function setElementAttribute(element, name, value, journal) {
    if (journal)
      journal.setAttribute(element, name, value);
    else
      element.setAttribute(name, value);
  }
  function removeElementAttribute(element, name, journal) {
    if (journal)
      journal.removeAttribute(element, name);
    else
      element.removeAttribute(name);
  }
  function canvasSnapshotsEqual(left, right) {
    if (!left || left.revision !== right.revision)
      return false;
    if (left.nodes.length !== right.nodes.length || left.edges.length !== right.edges.length)
      return false;
    return left.nodes.every((node, index) => canvasNodesEqual2(node, right.nodes[index])) && left.edges.every((edge, index) => canvasEdgesEqual2(edge, right.edges[index]));
  }
  function canvasNodesEqual2(left, right) {
    return right !== undefined && left.id === right.id && left.type === right.type && left.position.x === right.position.x && left.position.y === right.position.y && left.size?.width === right.size?.width && left.size?.height === right.size?.height && left.parentId === right.parentId && Object.is(left.data, right.data);
  }
  function canvasEdgesEqual2(left, right) {
    return right !== undefined && left.id === right.id && left.type === right.type && left.source.nodeId === right.source.nodeId && left.source.portId === right.source.portId && left.target.nodeId === right.target.nodeId && left.target.portId === right.target.portId && Object.is(left.data, right.data);
  }
  function validateChangeSetConsistency(commit) {
    const expected = collectGraphChanges(commit.before.snapshot, commit.after.snapshot);
    const actual = commit.changeSet.changes;
    if (expected.length === 0 && actual.length === 0) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Canvas Commit must contain a Change.", {
        issue: "INVALID_DOCUMENT_COMMIT"
      });
    }
    if (expected.length === actual.length && expected.every((change, index) => graphChangesEqual(change, actual[index]))) {
      return;
    }
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_DOCUMENT_UPDATE", "SVG Renderer Change Set does not match its before and after Views.", {
      issue: "CHANGE_SET_MISMATCH",
      beforeRevision: commit.changeSet.beforeRevision,
      revision: commit.changeSet.revision
    });
  }
  function collectGraphChanges(before, after) {
    const changes = [];
    const beforeNodes = new Map(before.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    const afterNodes = new Map(after.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    for (const id of sortedUnion(beforeNodes.keys(), afterNodes.keys())) {
      const beforeNode = beforeNodes.get(id) ?? null;
      const afterNode = afterNodes.get(id) ?? null;
      if (canvasNodeValuesEqual(beforeNode, afterNode))
        continue;
      changes.push({ entity: "node", id, before: beforeNode, after: afterNode });
    }
    const beforeEdges = new Map(before.edges.map((edge) => [edge.id, edge]));
    const afterEdges = new Map(after.edges.map((edge) => [edge.id, edge]));
    for (const id of sortedUnion(beforeEdges.keys(), afterEdges.keys())) {
      const beforeEdge = beforeEdges.get(id) ?? null;
      const afterEdge = afterEdges.get(id) ?? null;
      if (canvasEdgeValuesEqual(beforeEdge, afterEdge))
        continue;
      changes.push({ entity: "edge", id, before: beforeEdge, after: afterEdge });
    }
    return changes;
  }
  function sortedUnion(left, right) {
    return [...new Set([...left, ...right])].sort();
  }
  function graphChangesEqual(left, right) {
    if (!right || left.entity !== right.entity || left.id !== right.id)
      return false;
    if (left.entity === "node" && right.entity === "node") {
      return canvasNodeValuesEqual(left.before, right.before) && canvasNodeValuesEqual(left.after, right.after);
    }
    if (left.entity === "edge" && right.entity === "edge") {
      return canvasEdgeValuesEqual(left.before, right.before) && canvasEdgeValuesEqual(left.after, right.after);
    }
    return false;
  }
  function canvasNodeValuesEqual(left, right) {
    if (left === right)
      return true;
    if (left === null || right === null)
      return false;
    return canvasNodesEqual2(left, right);
  }
  function canvasEdgeValuesEqual(left, right) {
    if (left === right)
      return true;
    if (left === null || right === null)
      return false;
    return canvasEdgesEqual2(left, right);
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/interaction-projection.ts
  function acceptInteractionProjection(value, document2, ports, session) {
    if (!isRecord5(value))
      throwInvalidProjectionType();
    switch (value.type) {
      case "node-drag":
        return acceptNodeDrag(value, document2);
      case "viewport-pan":
        return acceptViewportPan(value, session);
      case "connection-preview":
        return acceptConnectionPreview(value, document2, ports);
      case "box-selection":
        return acceptBoxSelection(value);
      default:
        throwInvalidProjectionType();
    }
  }
  function isInteractionCompatibleWithDocument(interaction, document2, ports) {
    if (interaction?.type === "connection-preview") {
      if (!connectionAnchorAvailable(interaction.source, document2, ports))
        return false;
      return interaction.target.type === "none" || connectionAnchorAvailable(interaction.target.anchor, document2, ports);
    }
    if (interaction?.type !== "node-drag")
      return true;
    const nodes = new Map(document2.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    return interaction.nodes.every((candidate) => {
      const node = nodes.get(candidate.nodeId);
      return node !== undefined && node.position.x === candidate.basePosition.x && node.position.y === candidate.basePosition.y;
    });
  }
  function acceptNodeDrag(value, document2) {
    if (!Array.isArray(value.nodes))
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("nodes");
    for (let index = 0;index < value.nodes.length; index += 1) {
      const candidate = value.nodes[index];
      if (!isRecord5(candidate))
        throwInvalidProjectionStructure(`nodes[${index}]`);
      if (typeof candidate.nodeId !== "string" || candidate.nodeId.length === 0) {
        throwInvalidProjectionStructure(`nodes[${index}].nodeId`);
      }
      if (!isRecord5(candidate.basePosition))
        throwInvalidProjectionStructure(`nodes[${index}].basePosition`);
      if (!isRecord5(candidate.position))
        throwInvalidProjectionStructure(`nodes[${index}].position`);
    }
    const projection = value;
    if (projection.nodes.length === 0) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Node Drag Interaction Projection requires at least one Node.", { issue: "EMPTY_NODE_DRAG" });
    }
    const nodes = new Map(document2.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
    const seenNodeIds = new Set;
    let previousNodeId;
    const capturedPositions = new Map;
    for (const candidate of projection.nodes) {
      if (seenNodeIds.has(candidate.nodeId)) {
        throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection Node IDs must be unique.", {
          issue: "DUPLICATE_NODE"
        });
      }
      seenNodeIds.add(candidate.nodeId);
      if (previousNodeId !== undefined && previousNodeId > candidate.nodeId) {
        throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection Node IDs must use canonical order.", { issue: "NON_CANONICAL_NODE_ORDER" });
      }
      previousNodeId = candidate.nodeId;
      const baseSource = candidate.basePosition;
      const basePosition = { x: baseSource.x, y: baseSource.y };
      const positionSource = candidate.position;
      const position = { x: positionSource.x, y: positionSource.y };
      for (const [field, coordinate] of [
        ["basePosition.x", basePosition.x],
        ["basePosition.y", basePosition.y],
        ["position.x", position.x],
        ["position.y", position.y]
      ]) {
        if (Number.isFinite(coordinate))
          continue;
        throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection Node positions must be finite.", { issue: "INVALID_NODE_POSITION", field });
      }
      const node = nodes.get(candidate.nodeId);
      if (!node || node.position.x !== basePosition.x || node.position.y !== basePosition.y) {
        throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Interaction Projection Node Baseline is stale.", {
          issue: "NODE_POSITION_MISMATCH"
        });
      }
      capturedPositions.set(node.id, { baseSource, positionSource, basePosition, position });
    }
    return Object.freeze({
      type: "node-drag",
      nodes: Object.freeze(projection.nodes.map((candidate) => {
        const nodeId = candidate.nodeId;
        const captured = capturedPositions.get(nodeId);
        return Object.freeze({
          nodeId,
          basePosition: ownGeometry(captured.baseSource, captured.basePosition),
          position: ownGeometry(captured.positionSource, captured.position)
        });
      }))
    });
  }
  function acceptViewportPan(value, session) {
    if (!isRecord5(value.baseViewport))
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("baseViewport");
    if (!isRecord5(value.viewport))
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("viewport");
    const projection = value;
    const baseSource = projection.baseViewport;
    const baseViewport = captureViewport("baseViewport", baseSource);
    const viewportSource = projection.viewport;
    const viewport = captureViewport("viewport", viewportSource);
    if (!viewportsEqual(baseViewport, session.viewport)) {
      throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Interaction Projection Viewport Baseline is stale.", {
        issue: "VIEWPORT_MISMATCH"
      });
    }
    return Object.freeze({
      type: "viewport-pan",
      baseViewport: ownGeometry(baseSource, baseViewport),
      viewport: ownGeometry(viewportSource, viewport)
    });
  }
  function acceptConnectionPreview(value, document2, ports) {
    if (!isConnectionAnchor(value.source) || !isRecord5(value.pointerWorldPoint) || !isRecord5(value.target)) {
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("connection-preview");
    }
    if (value.target.type !== "none" && (value.target.type !== "valid" && value.target.type !== "invalid" || !isConnectionAnchor(value.target.anchor))) {
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("target");
    }
    const projection = value;
    const pointerSource = projection.pointerWorldPoint;
    const pointerWorldPoint = capturePoint("pointerWorldPoint", pointerSource);
    assertConnectionAnchorBaseline(projection.source, "source", document2, ports);
    if (projection.target.type !== "none")
      assertConnectionAnchorBaseline(projection.target.anchor, "target", document2, ports);
    return Object.freeze({
      type: "connection-preview",
      source: Object.freeze({ ...projection.source }),
      pointerWorldPoint: ownGeometry(pointerSource, pointerWorldPoint),
      target: projection.target.type === "none" ? Object.freeze({ type: "none" }) : Object.freeze({
        type: projection.target.type,
        anchor: Object.freeze({ ...projection.target.anchor })
      })
    });
  }
  function acceptBoxSelection(value) {
    if (!isRecord5(value.rect))
      throwInvalidProjectionStructure("rect");
    const projection = value;
    const rect = projection.rect;
    const read = (field) => {
      const coordinate = rect[field];
      if (Number.isFinite(coordinate) && (field !== "width" && field !== "height" || coordinate >= 0))
        return coordinate;
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Box Selection rect must be finite and non-negative.", {
        issue: "INVALID_BOX_SELECTION_RECT",
        field: "rect." + field
      });
    };
    const values = { x: read("x"), y: read("y"), width: read("width"), height: read("height") };
    return Object.freeze({ type: "box-selection", rect: ownGeometry(rect, values) });
  }
  function assertConnectionAnchorBaseline(anchor, role, document2, ports) {
    if (anchor.role !== role) {
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Connection Anchor role is invalid.", {
        issue: "INVALID_CONNECTION_ANCHOR_ROLE"
      });
    }
    if (connectionAnchorAvailable(anchor, document2, ports))
      return;
    throw new RendererError("INTERACTION_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Connection Anchor Node is unavailable.", {
      issue: "CONNECTION_ANCHOR_UNAVAILABLE"
    });
  }
  function capturePoint(field, point) {
    const read = (coordinate) => {
      const value = point[coordinate];
      if (Number.isFinite(value))
        return value;
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection Point must be finite.", {
        issue: "INVALID_POINT",
        field: field + "." + coordinate
      });
    };
    return { x: read("x"), y: read("y") };
  }
  function captureViewport(prefix, viewport) {
    const read = (field) => {
      const coordinate = viewport[field];
      if (Number.isFinite(coordinate) && (field !== "zoom" || coordinate > 0))
        return coordinate;
      throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection Viewport values must be finite with positive zoom.", { issue: "INVALID_VIEWPORT", field: prefix + "." + field });
    };
    return { x: read("x"), y: read("y"), zoom: read("zoom") };
  }
  function isConnectionAnchor(value) {
    return isRecord5(value) && typeof value.nodeId === "string" && value.nodeId.length > 0 && (value.portId === undefined || typeof value.portId === "string" && value.portId.length > 0) && (value.role === "source" || value.role === "target");
  }
  function connectionAnchorAvailable(anchor, document2, ports) {
    const node = document2.nodes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === anchor.nodeId);
    if (!node?.size || node.size.width <= 0 || node.size.height <= 0)
      return false;
    return isConnectionTargetDefined(ports, anchor);
  }
  function throwInvalidProjectionType() {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection type is invalid.", {
      issue: "INVALID_PROJECTION_TYPE"
    });
  }
  function throwInvalidProjectionStructure(field) {
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "Interaction Projection structure is invalid.", {
      issue: "INVALID_PROJECTION_STRUCTURE",
      field
    });
  }
  function isRecord5(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }
  function viewportsEqual(left, right) {
    return left.x === right.x && left.y === right.y && left.zoom === right.zoom;
  }
  function ownGeometry(source, values) {
    const snapshot = {};
    for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(source)) {
      const descriptor = Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(source, key);
      if (!descriptor?.enumerable)
        continue;
      Object.defineProperty(snapshot, key, {
        value: Object.hasOwn(values, key) ? Reflect.get(values, key) : Reflect.get(source, key),
        enumerable: true,
        configurable: true,
        writable: true
      });
    }
    return Object.freeze(Object.assign(snapshot, values));
  }

  // packages/renderer-svg/src/svg-renderer.ts
  var targetReservations = new WeakSet;
  function createSvgRenderer(config) {
    const { target, edgeHitTolerance, connectionAnchorHitTolerance } = validateConfig(config);
    const inputPolicies = normalizeInputPolicies(config.input);
    readTargetMatrix(target, "INVALID_TARGET");
    if (targetReservations.has(target) || target.querySelector(":scope > [data-nodebraid-renderer-svg-root]")) {
      throw new SvgRendererError("TARGET_OCCUPIED", "SVG Renderer Target is already reserved.");
    }
    const document2 = target.ownerDocument;
    const addedTabIndex = !target.hasAttribute("tabindex");
    if (addedTabIndex)
      target.setAttribute("tabindex", "-1");
    const projection = createSvgElement(document2, "g");
    projection.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg");
    projection.setAttribute("data-nodebraid-renderer-svg-root", "");
    const edgesLayer = createSvgElement(document2, "g");
    edgesLayer.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__edges");
    const nodesLayer = createSvgElement(document2, "g");
    nodesLayer.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__nodes");
    const interactionLayer = createSvgElement(document2, "g");
    interactionLayer.setAttribute("class", "nodebraid-renderer-svg__interaction");
    projection.append(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer);
    targetReservations.add(target);
    try {
      target.append(projection);
    } catch (error) {
      targetReservations.delete(target);
      if (addedTabIndex)
        target.removeAttribute("tabindex");
      throw error;
    }
    let disposed = false;
    let acceptedSession;
    let acceptedInteraction = null;
    let baselineSnapshot;
    let acceptedPorts;
    let projectionOutOfSync = false;
    let observedResizeError;
    let disposePromise;
    const inputListeners = new Set;
    const inputQueue = [];
    const activePointerIds = new Set;
    const capturedPointerIds = new Set;
    let inputDraining = false;
    const assertActive = () => {
      if (disposed)
        throw new RendererError("RENDERER_DISPOSED", "SVG Renderer Instance has been disposed.");
    };
    const throwProjectionOutOfSync = (receivedRevision) => {
      throw new RendererError("DOCUMENT_OUT_OF_SYNC", "SVG Renderer requires reset after DOM rollback failure.", {
        issue: "PROJECTION_ROLLBACK_FAILED",
        expectedRevision: baselineSnapshot?.revision ?? null,
        ...receivedRevision === undefined ? {} : { receivedRevision }
      });
    };
    const assertProjectionSynchronized = () => {
      if (projectionOutOfSync)
        throwProjectionOutOfSync();
    };
    const applySession = (snapshot, journal, acceptedTargetMatrix) => {
      const { x, y, zoom } = snapshot.viewport;
      const localToUser = acceptedTargetMatrix ?? readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
      const selectedNodeIds = new Set(snapshot.selection.nodeIds);
      const selectedEdgeIds = new Set(snapshot.selection.edgeIds);
      for (const node of nodesLayer.querySelectorAll("[data-nodebraid-node-id]")) {
        setSelected(node, selectedNodeIds.has(node.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-node-id") ?? ""), journal);
      }
      for (const edge of edgesLayer.querySelectorAll("[data-nodebraid-edge-id]")) {
        setSelected(edge, selectedEdgeIds.has(edge.getAttribute("data-nodebraid-edge-id") ?? ""), journal);
      }
      const matrix = {
        a: localToUser.a * zoom,
        b: localToUser.b * zoom,
        c: localToUser.c * zoom,
        d: localToUser.d * zoom,
        e: localToUser.a * x + localToUser.c * y + localToUser.e,
        f: localToUser.b * x + localToUser.d * y + localToUser.f
      };
      setElementAttribute(projection, "transform", formatSvgMatrix(matrix), journal);
    };
    const applyInteractionProjection = (previous, next, baseline, ports, session, journal, targetMatrix) => {
      applySession(session, journal, targetMatrix);
      if (previous?.type === "node-drag") {
        applyNodeDragProjection({
          type: "node-drag",
          nodes: baseline.nodes.map((node) => ({
            nodeId: node.id,
            basePosition: node.position,
            position: node.position
          }))
        }, baseline, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, ports, journal);
      }
      if (previous?.type === "connection-preview" && next?.type !== "connection-preview") {
        clearConnectionPreview(interactionLayer);
      }
      if (previous?.type === "box-selection" && next?.type !== "box-selection") {
        clearBoxSelection(interactionLayer);
      }
      switch (next?.type) {
        case "node-drag":
          applyNodeDragProjection(next, baseline, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, ports, journal);
          break;
        case "viewport-pan":
          applySession({ selection: session.selection, viewport: next.viewport }, journal, targetMatrix);
          break;
        case "connection-preview":
          applyConnectionPreview(next, baseline, ports, interactionLayer, journal);
          break;
        case "box-selection":
          applyBoxSelection(next, interactionLayer, journal);
          break;
      }
    };
    const refreshProjectionMapping = () => {
      assertProjectionSynchronized();
      const targetMatrix = readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
      if (!acceptedSession)
        return targetMatrix;
      const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer);
      try {
        applySession(createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction), journal, targetMatrix);
        return targetMatrix;
      } catch (error) {
        const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
        if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
          projectionOutOfSync = true;
          throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
        }
        throw error;
      }
    };
    const window2 = target.ownerDocument.defaultView;
    if (!window2?.ResizeObserver) {
      projection.remove();
      targetReservations.delete(target);
      if (addedTabIndex)
        target.removeAttribute("tabindex");
      throw new SvgRendererError("INVALID_TARGET", "SVG Renderer Target environment requires ResizeObserver.");
    }
    const resizeObserver = new window2.ResizeObserver(() => {
      if (disposed || !acceptedSession || projectionOutOfSync)
        return;
      const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer);
      try {
        applySession(createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction), journal);
        if (isTargetUnavailableError(observedResizeError))
          observedResizeError = undefined;
      } catch (error) {
        const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
        const nextError = rollbackErrors.length === 0 ? error : new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
        observedResizeError = observedResizeError === undefined || isTargetUnavailableError(observedResizeError) ? nextError : new AggregateError([observedResizeError, nextError], "Multiple SVG Renderer ResizeObserver updates failed.");
        if (rollbackErrors.length > 0)
          projectionOutOfSync = true;
      }
    });
    resizeObserver.observe(target);
    const assertNoObservedResizeError = () => {
      if (observedResizeError === undefined)
        return;
      const error = observedResizeError;
      if (isTargetUnavailableError(error)) {
        try {
          readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
        } catch {
          throw error;
        }
        observedResizeError = undefined;
        return;
      }
      observedResizeError = undefined;
      throw error;
    };
    const emitInput = (input, applyDisposition) => {
      if (applyDisposition) {
        let immediateDisposition;
        const immediateErrors = [];
        for (const subscription of Array.from(inputListeners)) {
          if (!subscription.decideDisposition)
            continue;
          try {
            const received = subscription.decideDisposition(input);
            if (received === undefined)
              continue;
            if (!isInputDisposition(received)) {
              throw new RendererError("INVALID_INPUT_DISPOSITION", "Renderer Input disposition is malformed.", {
                receivedType: typeof received
              });
            }
            immediateDisposition = received;
          } catch (error) {
            immediateErrors.push(error);
          }
        }
        if (immediateDisposition)
          applyDisposition(immediateDisposition);
        if (immediateErrors.length === 1)
          throw immediateErrors[0];
        if (immediateErrors.length > 1) {
          throw new AggregateError(immediateErrors, "SVG Renderer Input disposition listeners failed.");
        }
      }
      inputQueue.push({ input, applyDisposition });
      if (inputDraining)
        return;
      inputDraining = true;
      const errors = [];
      try {
        while (inputQueue.length > 0) {
          const queued = inputQueue.shift();
          if (!queued)
            continue;
          let disposition;
          for (const subscription of Array.from(inputListeners)) {
            try {
              const received = subscription.listener(queued.input);
              if (received === undefined)
                continue;
              if (!isInputDisposition(received)) {
                throw new RendererError("INVALID_INPUT_DISPOSITION", "Renderer Input disposition is malformed.", {
                  receivedType: typeof received
                });
              }
              disposition = received;
            } catch (error) {
              errors.push(error);
            }
          }
          if (disposition)
            queued.applyDisposition?.(disposition);
        }
      } finally {
        inputDraining = false;
      }
      if (errors.length === 1)
        throw errors[0];
      if (errors.length > 1)
        throw new AggregateError(errors, "SVG Renderer Input listeners failed.");
    };
    const handlePointer = (event) => {
      if (disposed)
        return;
      const endsPointer = event.type === "pointerup" || event.type === "pointercancel";
      let handlingFailed = false;
      let handlingError;
      try {
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        applyDomEventPolicy(event, inputPolicies.pointer);
        if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedSession) {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Input requires Document and Session state.", {
            issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE"
          });
        }
        refreshProjectionMapping();
        if (event.type === "pointerdown")
          activePointerIds.add(event.pointerId);
        emitInput(normalizePointerInput(event, target, createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction)), () => event.preventDefault());
      } catch (error) {
        handlingFailed = true;
        handlingError = error;
      }
      let cleanupFailed = false;
      let cleanupError;
      if (endsPointer) {
        try {
          releaseNativePointerCapture(target, event.pointerId, capturedPointerIds);
        } catch (error) {
          cleanupFailed = true;
          cleanupError = error;
        } finally {
          activePointerIds.delete(event.pointerId);
        }
      }
      if (handlingFailed && cleanupFailed) {
        throw new AggregateError([handlingError, cleanupError], "SVG Renderer Pointer handling and cleanup both failed.");
      }
      if (handlingFailed)
        throw handlingError;
      if (cleanupFailed)
        throw cleanupError;
    };
    for (const type of ["pointerdown", "pointermove", "pointerup", "pointercancel"]) {
      target.addEventListener(type, handlePointer);
    }
    const handleLostPointerCapture = (event) => {
      if (disposed || !capturedPointerIds.has(event.pointerId))
        return;
      capturedPointerIds.delete(event.pointerId);
      if (!activePointerIds.has(event.pointerId))
        return;
      try {
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedSession) {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Input requires Document and Session state.", {
            issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE"
          });
        }
        refreshProjectionMapping();
        emitInput(normalizePointerInput(event, target, createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction), "pointer.cancel"));
      } finally {
        activePointerIds.delete(event.pointerId);
      }
    };
    target.addEventListener("lostpointercapture", handleLostPointerCapture);
    const handleWheel = (event) => {
      if (disposed)
        return;
      assertNoObservedResizeError();
      assertProjectionSynchronized();
      applyDomEventPolicy(event, inputPolicies.wheel);
      if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedSession) {
        throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Input requires Document and Session state.", {
          issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE"
        });
      }
      refreshProjectionMapping();
      emitInput(normalizeWheelInput(event, target, createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction)), () => event.preventDefault());
    };
    const handleKeyboard = (event) => {
      if (disposed)
        return;
      assertNoObservedResizeError();
      assertProjectionSynchronized();
      applyDomEventPolicy(event, inputPolicies.keyboard);
      if (target.ownerDocument.activeElement !== target)
        return;
      if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedSession) {
        throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Input requires Document and Session state.", {
          issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE"
        });
      }
      refreshProjectionMapping();
      emitInput(normalizeKeyboardInput(event), () => event.preventDefault());
    };
    const handleContextMenu = (event) => {
      if (disposed)
        return;
      assertNoObservedResizeError();
      assertProjectionSynchronized();
      applyDomEventPolicy(event, inputPolicies.contextMenu);
    };
    const handleFocus = (event) => {
      if (disposed)
        return;
      assertNoObservedResizeError();
      assertProjectionSynchronized();
      emitInput(Object.freeze({ type: event.type === "focus" ? "focus.gained" : "focus.lost" }));
    };
    target.addEventListener("wheel", handleWheel, { passive: false });
    target.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyboard);
    target.addEventListener("keyup", handleKeyboard);
    target.addEventListener("contextmenu", handleContextMenu);
    target.addEventListener("focus", handleFocus);
    target.addEventListener("blur", handleFocus);
    const renderer = Object.freeze({
      updateDocument(update) {
        assertActive();
        const acceptedUpdate = validateRendererDocumentUpdate(update);
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        if (acceptedUpdate.type === "reset") {
          if (acceptedSession)
            validateSessionSnapshot(acceptedSession, acceptedUpdate.view.snapshot);
          const targetMatrix = readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
          const nextBaseline = cloneCanvasSnapshot(acceptedUpdate.view.snapshot);
          const nextPorts = acceptPortSnapshot(acceptedUpdate.ports, acceptedUpdate.view.snapshot);
          const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer);
          try {
            renderReset(acceptedUpdate.view, document2, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer, nextPorts);
            if (acceptedSession)
              applySession(acceptedSession, journal, targetMatrix);
          } catch (error) {
            const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
            if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
              projectionOutOfSync = true;
              throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
            }
            throw error;
          }
          baselineSnapshot = nextBaseline;
          acceptedPorts = nextPorts;
          acceptedInteraction = null;
          projectionOutOfSync = false;
          observedResizeError = undefined;
        } else {
          if (projectionOutOfSync)
            throwProjectionOutOfSync(acceptedUpdate.commit.before.snapshot.revision);
          const targetMatrix = readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
          const session = acceptedSession;
          const nextPorts = acceptPortSnapshot(acceptedUpdate.ports, acceptedUpdate.commit.after.snapshot);
          const nextInteraction = isInteractionCompatibleWithDocument(acceptedInteraction, acceptedUpdate.commit.after.snapshot, nextPorts) ? acceptedInteraction : null;
          try {
            applyCommit(acceptedUpdate.commit, baselineSnapshot, session, document2, edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer, nextPorts, (journal) => {
              if (session) {
                applyInteractionProjection(acceptedInteraction, nextInteraction, acceptedUpdate.commit.after.snapshot, nextPorts, session, journal, targetMatrix);
              }
            });
          } catch (error) {
            if (error instanceof ProjectionRollbackError)
              projectionOutOfSync = true;
            throw error;
          }
          baselineSnapshot = cloneCanvasSnapshot(acceptedUpdate.commit.after.snapshot);
          acceptedPorts = nextPorts;
          acceptedInteraction = nextInteraction;
        }
      },
      updateSession(snapshot) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedPorts) {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Session requires a Document Baseline.", {
            issue: "DOCUMENT_BASELINE_MISSING"
          });
        }
        validateSessionSnapshot(snapshot, baselineSnapshot);
        const accepted = cloneSessionSnapshot(snapshot);
        const nextInteraction = acceptedInteraction?.type === "viewport-pan" && (accepted.viewport.x !== acceptedInteraction.baseViewport.x || accepted.viewport.y !== acceptedInteraction.baseViewport.y || accepted.viewport.zoom !== acceptedInteraction.baseViewport.zoom) ? null : acceptedInteraction;
        const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer);
        try {
          applyInteractionProjection(acceptedInteraction, nextInteraction, baselineSnapshot, acceptedPorts, accepted, journal);
        } catch (error) {
          const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
          if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
            projectionOutOfSync = true;
            throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
          }
          throw error;
        }
        acceptedSession = accepted;
        acceptedInteraction = nextInteraction;
      },
      updateInteraction(projection) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedPorts || !acceptedSession) {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Interaction Projection requires Document and Session state.", { issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE" });
        }
        const accepted = projection === null ? null : acceptInteractionProjection(projection, baselineSnapshot, acceptedPorts, acceptedSession);
        const journal = new DomMutationJournal(edgesLayer, nodesLayer, interactionLayer);
        try {
          applyInteractionProjection(acceptedInteraction, accepted, baselineSnapshot, acceptedPorts, acceptedSession, journal);
        } catch (error) {
          const rollbackErrors = journal.rollback();
          if (rollbackErrors.length > 0) {
            projectionOutOfSync = true;
            throw new ProjectionRollbackError([error, ...rollbackErrors]);
          }
          throw error;
        }
        acceptedInteraction = accepted;
      },
      subscribeInput(listener, decideDisposition) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        if (typeof listener !== "function") {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_INPUT_SUBSCRIBER", "SVG Renderer Input listener must be a function.");
        }
        if (decideDisposition !== undefined && typeof decideDisposition !== "function") {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_INPUT_SUBSCRIBER", "SVG Renderer disposition listener must be a function.");
        }
        const subscription = { listener, decideDisposition };
        inputListeners.add(subscription);
        let subscribed = true;
        return () => {
          if (!subscribed)
            return;
          subscribed = false;
          inputListeners.delete(subscription);
        };
      },
      hitTest(point) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        if (!baselineSnapshot || !acceptedPorts || !acceptedSession) {
          throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "SVG Renderer Hit Test requires Document and Session state.", {
            issue: "RENDERER_STATE_INCOMPLETE"
          });
        }
        refreshProjectionMapping();
        return hitTestProjection(point, target, createEffectiveSnapshot(baselineSnapshot, acceptedInteraction), acceptedPorts, createEffectiveSession(acceptedSession, acceptedInteraction), edgeHitTolerance, connectionAnchorHitTolerance);
      },
      capturePointer(pointerId) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        assertActivePointer(pointerId, activePointerIds);
        if (capturedPointerIds.has(pointerId))
          return;
        target.setPointerCapture(pointerId);
        capturedPointerIds.add(pointerId);
      },
      releasePointer(pointerId) {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        assertActivePointer(pointerId, activePointerIds);
        releaseNativePointerCapture(target, pointerId, capturedPointerIds);
      },
      focus() {
        assertActive();
        assertNoObservedResizeError();
        assertProjectionSynchronized();
        readTargetMatrix(target, "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE");
        target.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        if (target.ownerDocument.activeElement !== target) {
          throw new SvgRendererError("TARGET_UNAVAILABLE", "SVG Renderer Target could not receive Focus.");
        }
      },
      dispose() {
        if (disposePromise)
          return disposePromise;
        disposed = true;
        inputQueue.length = 0;
        inputListeners.clear();
        disposePromise = Promise.resolve().then(() => {
          const cleanupErrors = [];
          for (const pointerId of Array.from(capturedPointerIds)) {
            attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => releaseNativePointerCapture(target, pointerId, capturedPointerIds));
          }
          activePointerIds.clear();
          for (const type of ["pointerdown", "pointermove", "pointerup", "pointercancel"]) {
            attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener(type, handlePointer));
          }
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("lostpointercapture", handleLostPointerCapture));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("wheel", handleWheel));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyboard));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("keyup", handleKeyboard));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("contextmenu", handleContextMenu));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("focus", handleFocus));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeEventListener("blur", handleFocus));
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => resizeObserver.disconnect());
          attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => projection.remove());
          if (addedTabIndex)
            attemptCleanup(cleanupErrors, () => target.removeAttribute("tabindex"));
          if (cleanupErrors.length > 0)
            throw new AggregateError(cleanupErrors, "SVG Renderer cleanup failed.");
          targetReservations.delete(target);
        });
        return disposePromise;
      }
    });
    return renderer;
  }
  function attemptCleanup(errors, cleanup) {
    try {
      cleanup();
    } catch (error) {
      errors.push(error);
    }
  }
  function isTargetUnavailableError(error) {
    return error instanceof SvgRendererError && error.code === "TARGET_UNAVAILABLE";
  }
  function isInputDisposition(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value))
      return false;
    const keys = Reflect.ownKeys(value);
    return keys.length === 1 && keys[0] === "defaultAction" && Reflect.get(value, "defaultAction") === "prevent";
  }
  function createEffectiveSnapshot(baseline, interaction) {
    if (interaction?.type !== "node-drag")
      return baseline;
    const candidates = new Map(interaction.nodes.map((candidate) => [candidate.nodeId, candidate.position]));
    return {
      revision: baseline.revision,
      nodes: baseline.nodes.map((node) => {
        const position = candidates.get(node.id);
        return position ? { ...node, position } : node;
      }),
      edges: baseline.edges
    };
  }
  function createEffectiveSession(session, interaction) {
    return interaction?.type === "viewport-pan" ? { selection: session.selection, viewport: interaction.viewport } : session;
  }
  // packages/renderer-svg/browser-tests/interaction-geometry-values.entry.ts
  var slots = {
    "node-drag": [
      { slot: "basePosition", field: "x" },
      { slot: "basePosition", field: "y" },
      { slot: "position", field: "x" },
      { slot: "position", field: "y" }
    ],
    "viewport-pan": [
      { slot: "baseViewport", field: "x" },
      { slot: "baseViewport", field: "y" },
      { slot: "baseViewport", field: "zoom" },
      { slot: "viewport", field: "x" },
      { slot: "viewport", field: "y" },
      { slot: "viewport", field: "zoom" }
    ],
    "box-selection": ["x", "y", "width", "height"].map((field) => ({ slot: "rect", field })),
    "connection-preview": ["x", "y"].map((field) => ({ slot: "pointerWorldPoint", field }))
  };
  function check(value, message) {
    if (!value)
      throw new Error(message);
  }
  function equal(actual, expected, message) {
    check(JSON.stringify(actual) === JSON.stringify(expected), message + ": " + JSON.stringify(actual));
  }
  function attributes(target, selector, keys) {
    const element = target.querySelector(selector);
    check(element !== null, "Expected public SVG element " + selector);
    return Object.fromEntries(keys.map((key) => [key, element.getAttribute(key)]));
  }
  async function geometryCase(family, shape, focus, mode, original) {
    const target = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    target.setAttribute("width", "400");
    target.setAttribute("height", "300");
    document.body.append(target);
    const renderer = createSvgRenderer({ target }), kernel = createCanvasKernel();
    const a = nodeId("a"), b = nodeId("b"), e = edgeId("edge");
    kernel.transact((tx) => {
      tx.nodes.add({ id: a, type: "task", position: { x: 10, y: 20 }, size: { width: 80, height: 40 }, data: null });
      tx.nodes.add({ id: b, type: "task", position: { x: 220, y: 40 }, size: { width: 100, height: 60 }, data: null });
      tx.edges.add({ id: e, type: "link", source: { nodeId: a }, target: { nodeId: b }, data: null });
    });
    const view = kernel.read();
    const sources = new Map, reads = new Map, metadata = new Map;
    const opaque = { caller: Symbol("metadata") };
    function geometry(slot, values) {
      const descriptors = {};
      for (const field of Object.keys(values)) {
        descriptors[field] = {
          enumerable: shape === "own",
          configurable: true,
          get() {
            check(this === sources.get(slot), "Geometry getter original receiver");
            const key = slot + "." + field, count = (reads.get(key) ?? 0) + 1;
            reads.set(key, count);
            if (mode === "first-error-order") {
              if (slot === focus.slot && field === focus.field)
                return Number.NaN;
              if (family === "node-drag" && slot === "position" && field === "y" || family !== "node-drag" && slot === focus.slot && field === "y")
                throw original;
            }
            if (slot === focus.slot && field === focus.field) {
              if (mode === "throw")
                throw original;
              if (mode === "invalid-first")
                return count === 1 ? Number.NaN : Reflect.get(values, field);
              if (mode === "mutate" && count > 1)
                return Number.NaN;
            }
            return Reflect.get(values, field);
          }
        };
      }
      const source = shape === "inherited" ? Object.create(Object.defineProperties({}, descriptors)) : Object.defineProperties({}, descriptors);
      sources.set(slot, source);
      Object.defineProperty(source, "metadata", {
        enumerable: true,
        get() {
          check(this === source, "Metadata getter original receiver");
          metadata.set(slot, (metadata.get(slot) ?? 0) + 1);
          if (mode === "metadata-throw" && slot === focus.slot)
            throw original;
          return opaque;
        }
      });
      return source;
    }
    let projection;
    switch (family) {
      case "node-drag":
        projection = {
          type: family,
          nodes: [
            {
              nodeId: a,
              basePosition: geometry("basePosition", { x: 10, y: 20 }),
              position: geometry("position", { x: 80, y: 90 })
            }
          ]
        };
        break;
      case "viewport-pan":
        projection = {
          type: family,
          baseViewport: geometry("baseViewport", { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 }),
          viewport: geometry("viewport", { x: 20, y: 30, zoom: 2 })
        };
        break;
      case "box-selection":
        projection = { type: family, rect: geometry("rect", { x: 5, y: 6, width: 70, height: 80 }) };
        break;
      case "connection-preview":
        projection = {
          type: family,
          source: { nodeId: a, role: "source" },
          pointerWorldPoint: geometry("pointerWorldPoint", { x: 330, y: 140 }),
          target: { type: "none" }
        };
        break;
    }
    try {
      renderer.updateDocument({ type: "reset", view });
      renderer.updateSession({ selection: { nodeIds: [a], edgeIds: [] }, viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 } });
      const node = target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]');
      const documentDOM = target.innerHTML;
      renderer.updateInteraction({ type: "box-selection", rect: { x: 2, y: 3, width: 20, height: 30 } });
      const previousDOM = target.innerHTML;
      let didThrow = false, caught;
      try {
        renderer.updateInteraction(projection);
      } catch (error) {
        didThrow = true;
        caught = error;
      }
      if (mode === "mutate") {
        check(!didThrow, "Valid captured geometry must project");
        for (const candidate of slots[family])
          check(reads.get(candidate.slot + "." + candidate.field) === 1, "Every declared coordinate read once");
        for (const [slot, source] of sources) {
          check(metadata.get(slot) === 1, "Own enumerable metadata copied once");
          check(!Object.isFrozen(source), "Caller geometry remains mutable");
          Object.defineProperty(source, "callerMutation", { value: true });
        }
        check(!Object.isFrozen(opaque), "Caller metadata remains shallow");
        switch (family) {
          case "node-drag":
            equal(attributes(target, '[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]', ["x", "y"]), { x: "80", y: "90" }, "Captured Node position");
            equal(attributes(target, '[data-nodebraid-edge-id="edge"]', ["x1", "y1", "x2", "y2"]), { x1: "120", y1: "110", x2: "270", y2: "70" }, "Incident Edge follows captured position");
            equal(renderer.hitTest({ x: 100, y: 100 }), { type: "node", nodeId: a, worldPoint: { x: 100, y: 100 } }, "Hit uses effective candidate geometry");
            break;
          case "viewport-pan":
            equal(attributes(target, "[data-nodebraid-renderer-svg-root]", ["transform"]), { transform: "matrix(2 0 0 2 20 30)" }, "Captured Viewport transform");
            equal(renderer.hitTest({ x: 120, y: 110 }), { type: "node", nodeId: a, worldPoint: { x: 50, y: 40 } }, "Hit uses effective candidate Viewport");
            break;
          case "box-selection":
            equal(attributes(target, "[data-nodebraid-box-selection]", ["x", "y", "width", "height"]), { x: "5", y: "6", width: "70", height: "80" }, "Captured rectangle");
            break;
          case "connection-preview":
            equal(attributes(target, "[data-nodebraid-connection-preview]", ["x1", "y1", "x2", "y2"]), { x1: "90", y1: "40", x2: "330", y2: "140" }, "Captured pointer endpoint");
            break;
        }
        for (const candidate of slots[family])
          check(reads.get(candidate.slot + "." + candidate.field) === 1, "Hit does not re-read caller geometry");
      } else {
        check(didThrow, "Invalid or throwing geometry must throw");
        if (mode === "invalid-first" || mode === "first-error-order" && family !== "node-drag") {
          check(caught instanceof RendererError && caught.code === "INVALID_INTERACTION_PROJECTION", "First invalid value remains rejected");
          check(reads.get(focus.slot + "." + focus.field) === 1, "No retry of invalid first value");
        } else
          check(caught === original, "Original thrown value identity");
        if (mode === "first-error-order") {
          const later = family === "node-drag" ? "position.y" : focus.slot + ".y";
          check((reads.get(later) ?? 0) === (family === "node-drag" ? 1 : 0), "Original eager/sequential first-error prefix retained");
        }
        check(target.innerHTML === previousDOM, "Rejected replacement retains previous accepted DOM");
      }
      check(kernel.read() === view, "Renderer cannot write the Kernel baseline");
      renderer.updateInteraction(null);
      check(target.innerHTML === documentDOM, "Clear restores committed geometry and Session");
      check(target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]') === node, "Keyed Node identity survives replacement/clear");
    } finally {
      await renderer.dispose();
      check(target.childElementCount === 0 && !target.hasAttribute("tabindex"), "Owned subtree and temporary tabindex removed");
      target.remove();
    }
  }
  async function runInteractionGeometryValueCases() {
    const results = [];
    const run = async (id, execute) => {
      try {
        await execute();
        results.push({ id, passed: true });
      } catch (error) {
        results.push({ id, passed: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error) });
      }
    };
    const failures = [undefined, null, false, 0, "", Symbol("geometry-read"), new Error("same identity")];
    for (const family of Object.keys(slots)) {
      for (const focus of slots[family]) {
        for (const shape of ["own", "inherited", "non-enumerable"]) {
          await run("captured/" + family + "/" + focus.slot + "." + focus.field + "/" + shape, () => geometryCase(family, shape, focus, "mutate"));
        }
        for (const [index, original] of failures.entries()) {
          await run("raw-error/" + family + "/" + focus.slot + "." + focus.field + "/" + index, () => geometryCase(family, "own", focus, "throw", original));
        }
        await run("invalid-first/" + family + "/" + focus.slot + "." + focus.field, () => geometryCase(family, "inherited", focus, "invalid-first"));
      }
      await run("first-error-order/" + family, () => geometryCase(family, "own", slots[family][0], "first-error-order", new Error("later coordinate")));
      for (const [index, original] of failures.entries()) {
        const focus = slots[family][0];
        await run("metadata-error/" + family + "/" + index, () => geometryCase(family, "own", focus, "metadata-throw", original));
      }
    }
    results.push(...(await runInteractionGeometryArrayCases()).results);
    results.push(...(await runInteractionGeometryMetadataCases()).results);
    return {
      controls: results.length,
      passed: results.filter((result) => result.passed).length,
      results,

  async function geometryArrayCase(cloneIterator) {
    const target = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    target.setAttribute("width", "600");
    target.setAttribute("height", "400");
    document.body.append(target);

    });

        return [{ ...this[0] }, { ...this[1] }].values();
      };

      renderer.updateSession({ selection: { nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] }, viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 } });
      const before = target.innerHTML, nodeA = target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]'), nodeB = target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="b"]');
      renderer.updateInteraction({ type: "node-drag", nodes });

      equal(attributes(target, '[data-nodebraid-node-id="b"]', ["x", "y"]), { x: "280", y: "190" }, "Node b keeps its validated candidate");
      equal(renderer.hitTest({ x: 100, y: 100 }), { type: "node", nodeId: a, worldPoint: { x: 100, y: 100 } }, "Candidate a hit");
      equal(renderer.hitTest({ x: 310, y: 210 }), { type: "node", nodeId: b, worldPoint: { x: 310, y: 210 } }, "Candidate b hit");
      check(kernel.read() === view, "Array projection leaves Kernel unchanged");
      renderer.updateInteraction(null);
      check(target.innerHTML === before, "Array projection clear restores baseline");
      check(target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]') === nodeA && target.querySelector('[data-nodebraid-node-id="b"]') === nodeB, "Both keyed Node elements survive");
    } finally {
      await renderer.dispose();
      check(target.childElementCount === 0 && !target.hasAttribute("tabindex"), "Array case cleanup");
      target.remove();
    }
  }

  async function geometryMetadataCase(slot, mode) {
    const target = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    target.setAttribute("width", "400");
    target.setAttribute("height", "300");
    document.body.append(target);
    const renderer = createSvgRenderer({ target }), kernel = createCanvasKernel(), id = nodeId("a");
    kernel.transact((tx) => tx.nodes.add({ id, type: "task", position: { x: 10, y: 20 }, size: { width: 80, height: 40 }, data: null }));
    const original = new Error(slot + "/" + mode), opaque = { caller: Symbol(mode) };
    let rawReads = 0, cloneReads = 0;
    const rawPoint = { x: slot === "basePosition" ? 10 : 80, y: slot === "basePosition" ? 20 : 90 };
    Object.defineProperty(rawPoint, "metadata", {
      enumerable: true,
      get() {
        check(this === rawPoint, "Original map Point metadata receiver");
        rawReads += 1;
        if (mode === "raw-throws")
          throw original;
        return opaque;
      }
    });
    const clonePoint = { x: rawPoint.x, y: rawPoint.y };
    Object.defineProperty(clonePoint, "metadata", {
      enumerable: true,
      get() {
        cloneReads += 1;
        if (mode === "clone-throws")
          throw original;
        return opaque;
      }
    });
    const candidate = {
      nodeId: id,
      basePosition: slot === "basePosition" ? rawPoint : { x: 10, y: 20 },
      position: slot === "position" ? rawPoint : { x: 80, y: 90 }
    };
    const clone = { ...candidate, [slot]: clonePoint };
    const nodes = [candidate];
    nodes[Symbol.iterator] = function() {
      return [clone].values();
    };
    try {
      const view = kernel.read();
      renderer.updateDocument({ type: "reset", view });
      renderer.updateSession({ selection: { nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] }, viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 } });
      const documentDOM = target.innerHTML;
      renderer.updateInteraction({ type: "box-selection", rect: { x: 2, y: 3, width: 20, height: 30 } });
      const previousDOM = target.innerHTML;
      let didThrow = false, caught;
      try {
        renderer.updateInteraction({ type: "node-drag", nodes });
      } catch (error) {
        didThrow = true;
        caught = error;
      }
      check(rawReads === 1 && cloneReads === 0, "Copy stage reads original map metadata, not validation iterator clone");
      if (mode === "raw-throws") {
        check(didThrow && caught === original, "Original map metadata error identity retained");
        check(target.innerHTML === previousDOM, "Metadata error preserves accepted DOM");
      } else {
        check(!didThrow, "Uncopied iterator metadata cannot introduce an error");
        equal(attributes(target, '[data-nodebraid-node-id="a"]', ["x", "y"]), { x: "80", y: "90" }, "Captured numeric geometry still projects");
      }
      check(kernel.read() === view && !Object.isFrozen(opaque), "Kernel baseline and shallow caller metadata retained");
      renderer.updateInteraction(null);
      check(target.innerHTML === documentDOM, "Metadata case clears to committed DOM");
    } finally {
      await renderer.dispose();
      check(target.childElementCount === 0 && !target.hasAttribute("tabindex"), "Metadata case cleanup");
      target.remove();
    }
  }

  var current;
  function createPolicyTarget() {
    const outer = document.createElement("div");
    outer.style.position = "fixed";
    outer.style.inset = "0";
    document.body.append(outer);
    const target = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    target.setAttribute("width", "400");
    target.setAttribute("height", "300");
    outer.append(target);
    return { outer, target };
  }
  function setupPolicyCase(family, flag, mode, kind) {
    if (current)
      throw new Error("Previous policy case was not cleaned up.");
    const { outer, target } = createPolicyTarget();
    const policy = { preventDefault: false, stopPropagation: false };
    let reads = 0;
    let receiverCorrect = true;
    const holder = kind === "inherited" ? Object.create(Object.getPrototypeOf(policy)) : policy;
    if (kind === "inherited")
      Reflect.deleteProperty(policy, flag);
    Object.defineProperty(holder, flag, {
      enumerable: kind !== "non-enumerable",
      get() {
        receiverCorrect &&= this === policy;
        reads += 1;
        if (reads > 1)
          throw new Error("Policy value was read after capture.");
        return mode === "first-absent" ? undefined : mode === "first-true";
      }
    });
    if (kind === "inherited")
      Object.setPrototypeOf(policy, holder);
    const renderer = createSvgRenderer({ target, input: { [family]: policy } });
    const kernel = createCanvasKernel();
    renderer.updateDocument({ type: "reset", view: kernel.read() });
    renderer.updateSession({ selection: { nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] }, viewport: { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 } });
    const inputs = [];
    const stop = renderer.subscribeInput((input) => {
      inputs.push(input);
    });
    renderer.focus();
    let resolve;
    const ready = new Promise((complete) => {
      resolve = complete;
    });
    let targetEvent;
    let bubbled = 0;
    const eventType = family === "pointer" ? "pointerdown" : family === "keyboard" ? "keydown" : family === "contextMenu" ? "contextmenu" : "wheel";
    target.addEventListener(eventType, (event) => {
      targetEvent = {
        isTrusted: event.isTrusted,
        defaultPrevented: event.defaultPrevented,
        cancelBubble: event.cancelBubble
      };
      resolve();
    }, { passive: true });
    outer.addEventListener(eventType, () => {
      bubbled += 1;
    }, { passive: true });
    const inputType = family === "pointer" ? "pointer.down" : family === "keyboard" ? "key.down" : family === "wheel" ? "wheel" : null;
    current = {
      renderer,
      outer,
      stop,
      ready,
      read: () => ({
        case: [family, flag, mode, kind].join(":"),
        reads,
        receiverCorrect,
        targetEvent,
        bubbled,
        semanticInputs: inputType === null ? 0 : inputs.filter((input) => input.type === inputType).length
      })
    };
    const bounds = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: bounds.left + 80, y: bounds.top + 80 };
  }
  async function readPolicyCase() {
    if (!current)
      throw new Error("Expected a policy case.");
    await current.ready;
    await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve()));
    return current.read();
  }
  async function cleanupPolicyCase() {
    if (!current)
      throw new Error("Expected a policy case to clean up.");
    const owned = current;
    current = undefined;
    owned.stop();
    await owned.renderer.dispose();
    owned.outer.remove();
  }
  async function policyConstructorFailure(family, flag, scenario) {
    const { outer, target } = createPolicyTarget();
    const caller = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "defs");
    target.append(caller);
    const before = target.outerHTML;
    const policy = { preventDefault: false, stopPropagation: false };
    let reads = 0;
    let receiverCorrect = true;
    let laterFlagReads = 0;
    const originals = [undefined, null, false, 0, "", Symbol("original policy read")];
    const throws = scenario.startsWith("throw-");
    const original = originals[Number(scenario.slice("throw-".length))];
    if (flag === "preventDefault") {
      Object.defineProperty(policy, "stopPropagation", {
        enumerable: true,
        get() {
          laterFlagReads += 1;
          throw new Error("First policy failure must precede the later field.");
        }
      });
    }
    Object.defineProperty(policy, flag, {
      enumerable: true,
      get() {
        receiverCorrect &&= this === policy;
        reads += 1;
        if (throws)
          throw original;
        if (reads > 1)
          return true;
        return scenario === "invalid-null" ? null : scenario === "invalid-number" ? 0 : "yes";
      }
    });
    let renderer;
    let received;
    let didThrow = false;
    try {
      renderer = createSvgRenderer({ target, input: { [family]: policy } });
    } catch (error) {
      received = error;
      didThrow = true;
    }
    const result = {
      case: [family, flag, scenario].join(":"),
      reads,
      receiverCorrect,
      laterFlagReads,
      code: typeof received === "object" && received !== null && "code" in received ? String(received.code) : null,
      field: typeof received === "object" && received !== null && "details" in received ? received.details.field : null,
      originalError: throws && didThrow && Object.is(received, original),
      targetUnchanged: target.outerHTML === before
    };
    await renderer?.dispose();
    const replacement = createSvgRenderer({ target });
    await replacement.dispose();
    const reusable = target.contains(caller) && target.outerHTML === before;
    outer.remove();
    return { ...result, reusable };
  }

  var commandIds = new WeakMap;
  function defineCommand(id) {
    if (typeof id !== "string") {
      throw new CommandError("INVALID_COMMAND", "Command ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ receivedType: id === null ? "null" : typeof id }));
    }
    if (id.length === 0) {
      throw new CommandError("INVALID_COMMAND", "Command ID must be a non-empty string.", Object.freeze({ commandId: id }));
    }
    const command = Object.freeze({ id });
    commandIds.set(command, id);
    return command;
  }
  function getCommandId(command) {
    const commandId = typeof command === "object" && command !== null ? commandIds.get(command) : undefined;
    if (commandId === undefined) {
      throw new CommandError("INVALID_COMMAND", "Command must be created with defineCommand().");
    }
    return commandId;
  }

  function tokenize(source, delimiters, delimiter) {
    const output = [];
    let state = 0;
    for (let i = 0;i < source.length; i++) {
      const code = source.charCodeAt(i);
      if (code >= 65 && code <= 90) {
        if (state === 1) {
          const next = source.charCodeAt(i + 1);
          if (next >= 97 && next <= 122) {
            output.push(delimiter);
          }
          output.push(code + 32);
        } else {
          if (state !== 0) {
            output.push(delimiter);
          }
          output.push(code + 32);
        }
        state = 1;
      } else if (code >= 97 && code <= 122) {
        output.push(code);
        state = 2;
      } else if (delimiters.includes(code)) {
        if (state !== 0) {
          output.push(delimiter);
        }
        state = 0;
      } else {
        output.push(code);
      }
    }
    return String.fromCharCode(...output);
  }

    return tokenize(source, [45, 95], 45);
  }

        hostId: this.#hostId,
        installationId,
        ...pluginName === undefined ? {} : { pluginName }
      });
      return Object.freeze({
        diagnostics: this.#createPluginDiagnostics(installationScope),
        findUndiagnosedError: (error) => this.#findUndiagnosedError(error, new Set),
        createActivation: () => this.#createPluginDiagnostics(Object.freeze({
          ...installationScope,
          activationId: `${this.#hostId}.activation.${++this.#nextActivationId}`
        }))
      });
    }
    #createPluginDiagnostics(scope) {
      return Object.freeze({
        emit: (input) => {
          this.#emit(scope, input, false);
        },
        reportFault: (error, input) => {
          let name;
          try {
            name = input.name;
            const event = this.#emit(scope, { name, attributes: input.attributes, level: "error", error }, true);
            this.#reportFault(Object.freeze({ event, error }));
          } catch (contractError) {
            const reportedError = new AggregateError([error, contractError], "Fault reporting input was invalid.");
            const event = this.#createEvent(scope, {
              name: diagnosticEvents.faultReportingFault,
              level: "error",
              attributes: {
                ...typeof name === "string" ? { attemptedEventName: name } : {
                  attemptedEventType: name === null ? "null" : typeof name
                },
                contractCode: contractError instanceof DiagnosticsError ? contractError.code : "UNKNOWN_CONTRACT_FAILURE"
              },
              error: reportedError
            });
            this.#reportFault(Object.freeze({ event, error: reportedError }));
          }
        }
      });
    }
    #emit(scope, input, required) {
      const name = input.name;
      if (typeof name !== "string" || name.length === 0) {
        throw new DiagnosticsError("INVALID_EVENT", "Diagnostic Event name must be a non-empty string.", typeof name === "string" ? { field: "name", value: name } : { field: "name", receivedType: name === null ? "null" : typeof name });
      }
      const level = input.level;
      if (!["debug", "info", "warn", "error"].includes(level)) {
        throw new DiagnosticsError("INVALID_EVENT", "Diagnostic Event level must be debug, info, warn, or error.", typeof level === "string" ? { field: "level", value: level } : { field: "level", receivedType: level === null ? "null" : typeof level });
      }
      let attributes;
      try {
        attributes = normalizeDiagnosticAttributes(input.attributes ?? {});
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof DiagnosticValueError) {
          throw new DiagnosticsError("INVALID_DIAGNOSTIC_VALUE", "Diagnostic Event attributes must be JSON-safe.", error.details, { cause: error });
        }
        throw error;
      }
      if (!this.#sink && !required)
        return;
      const event = this.#createEvent(scope, { name, level, attributes, error: input.error });
      if (this.#sink) {
        try {
          const result = this.#sink(event);
          if (isPromiseLike(result)) {
            this.#reportSinkFault(scope, name, new DiagnosticsError("ASYNC_SINK", "Diagnostic Sink must return synchronously.", { eventName: name }));
            Promise.resolve(result).catch((sinkError) => {
              this.#reportSinkFault(scope, name, sinkError);
            });
          }
        } catch (sinkError) {
          this.#reportSinkFault(scope, name, sinkError);
        }
      }
      return event;
    }
    #createEvent(scope, input) {
      const sequence = ++this.#sequence;
      if (input.level === "error" && Object.hasOwn(input, "error") && isReference(input.error)) {
        this.#diagnosedErrors.add(input.error);
      }
      return Object.freeze({
        version: 1,
        id: `${this.#hostId}.event.${sequence}`,
        sequence,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        name: input.name,
        level: input.level,
        scope,
        attributes: normalizeDiagnosticAttributes(input.attributes ?? {}),
        ...input.error === undefined ? {} : { error: input.error }
      });
    }
    #reportFault(fault) {
      try {
        const result = this.#faultReporter(fault);
        if (isPromiseLike(result)) {
          const contractError = new DiagnosticsError("ASYNC_FAULT_REPORTER", "Fault Reporter must return synchronously.", { eventName: fault.event.name }, { cause: fault.error });
          Promise.resolve(result).then(() => this.#scheduleFinalError(contractError), (reporterError) => this.#scheduleFinalError(new AggregateError([fault.error, contractError, reporterError], "Asynchronous Fault Reporter failed.")));
        }
      } catch (reporterError) {
        this.#scheduleFinalError(new AggregateError([fault.error, reporterError], "Fault reporting failed."));
      }
    }
    #scheduleFinalError(error) {
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        throw error;
      });
    }
    #reportSinkFault(scope, eventName, error) {
      const faultEvent = this.#createEvent(scope, {
        name: diagnosticEvents.sinkFault,
        level: "error",
        attributes: normalizeDiagnosticAttributes({ eventName }),
        error
      });
      this.#reportFault(Object.freeze({ event: faultEvent, error }));
    }
    #findUndiagnosedError(error, ancestors) {
      if (isReference(error) && this.#diagnosedErrors.has(error)) {
        return Object.freeze({ found: false });
      }
      if (!(error instanceof AggregateError) || ancestors.has(error)) {
        return Object.freeze({ found: true, error });
      }
      ancestors.add(error);
      try {
        const members = error.errors;
        if (!Array.isArray(members))
          throw new TypeError("AggregateError.errors must be an array.");
        const nestedErrors = Array.from({ length: members.length });
        const length = nestedErrors.length;
        let retained = 0;
        for (let index = 0;index < length; index += 1) {
          if (!(index in members))
            continue;
          const result = this.#findUndiagnosedError(members[index], ancestors);
          if (result.found)
            nestedErrors[retained++] = result.error;
        }
        nestedErrors.length = retained;
        if (nestedErrors.length === 0)
          return Object.freeze({ found: false });
        if (nestedErrors.length === length && nestedErrors.every((nestedError, index) => nestedError === members[index])) {
          return Object.freeze({ found: true, error });
        }
        return Object.freeze({
          found: true,
          error: new AggregateError(nestedErrors, error.message)
        });
      } finally {
        ancestors.delete(error);
      }
    }
  }
  function reportFaultToPlatform(fault) {
    if (typeof globalThis.reportError === "function") {
      globalThis.reportError(fault.error);
      return;
    }
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      throw fault.error;
    });
  }
  function isPromiseLike(value) {
    return isReference(value) && typeof Reflect.get(value, "then") === "function";
  }
  function isReference(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null || typeof value === "function";
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plugin-host-error.js
  class PluginHostError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "PluginHostError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("runtime.plugin-host", code, message, {
        details,
        cause: options?.cause
      });
    }
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plain-record.js
  function inspectPlainRecord(value, arrayStatus) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== "object")
      return { status: "invalid-value" };
    if (Array.isArray(value))
      return { status: arrayStatus };
    const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
    if (prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) {
      return { status: "invalid-prototype" };
    }
    const keys = [];
    for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
      if (typeof key !== "string")
        return { status: "invalid-key", key };
      keys.push(key);
    }
    return { status: "valid", record: value, keys };
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/service-token.js
  var nextServiceId = 0;
  var serviceNames = new WeakMap;
  function defineService(name) {
    const token = Object.freeze({ name });
    serviceNames.set(token, `nodebraid.service.${++nextServiceId}`);
    return token;
  }
  function getServiceName(token) {
    const name = serviceNames.get(token);
    if (!name) {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", "Service Token must be created with defineService().");
    }
    return name;
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plugin-definition.js
  var internalPlugins = new WeakMap;
  function definePlugin(definition) {
    if (!definition || typeof definition !== "object") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", "Plugin definition must be an object with a setup function.");
    }
    const name = definition.name;
    const setup = definition.setup;
    const requiresInput = definition.requires;
    const providesInput = definition.provides;
    if (name !== undefined && typeof name !== "string") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", "Plugin definition name must be a string when provided.");
    }
    if (typeof setup !== "function") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", "Plugin definition must include a setup function.");
    }
    const requires = normalizeServiceBindings(requiresInput, "requires");
    const provides = normalizeServiceBindings(providesInput, "provides");
    const requiredTokens = new Set(Object.values(requires));
    for (const token of Object.values(provides)) {
      if (requiredTokens.has(token)) {
        throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", `Plugin "${name ?? "<anonymous>"}" cannot require and provide Service Token "${token.name}".`);
      }
    }
    const frozenDefinition = Object.freeze({
      name,
      requires,
      provides,
      setup
    });
    const plugin = Object.freeze({ name, requires, provides });
    internalPlugins.set(plugin, { definition: frozenDefinition });
    return plugin;
  }
  function getInternalPlugin(plugin) {
    return internalPlugins.get(plugin);
  }
  function normalizeServiceBindings(bindings, field) {
    if (bindings === undefined)
      return Object.freeze({});
    const inspection = inspectPlainRecord(bindings, "invalid-value");
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-value") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", `Plugin ${field} must be a Service Binding record.`);
    }
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-prototype") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", `Plugin ${field} must be a plain Service Binding record.`);
    }
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-key") {
      throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", `Plugin ${field} must contain only enumerable string Service Bindings.`);
    }
    const normalized = {};
    for (const binding of inspection.keys) {
      const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(inspection.record, binding);
      if (descriptor === undefined || !descriptor.enumerable) {
        throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", `Plugin ${field} must contain only enumerable string Service Bindings.`);
      }
      const token = Reflect.get(inspection.record, binding);
      getServiceName(token);
      Object.defineProperty(normalized, binding, {
        enumerable: true,
        value: token
      });
    }
    return Object.freeze(normalized);
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plugin-graph.js
  class PluginGraph {
    #nodes = new Set;
    #providerReservations = new Map;
    register(pluginName, requires, provides) {
      const node = { pluginName, requires, provides };
      const providedTokens = [...new Set(Object.values(provides))];
      for (const token of providedTokens) {
        const existing = this.#providerReservations.get(token);
        if (existing) {
          throw new PluginHostError("PROVIDER_CONFLICT", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" cannot provide Service Token "${token.name}" because it is reserved by "${existing.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}".`, {
            type: "provider-conflict",
            serviceName: token.name,
            existingProvider: existing.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>",
            conflictingProvider: pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"
          });
        }
      }
      const candidateProviders = new Map(this.#providerReservations);
      for (const token of providedTokens)
        candidateProviders.set(token, node);
      const cycle = findDependencyCycle([...this.#nodes, node], candidateProviders);
      if (cycle) {
        throw new PluginHostError("DEPENDENCY_CYCLE", formatDependencyCycle(cycle), createDependencyCycleDetails(cycle));
      }
      this.#nodes.add(node);
      for (const token of providedTokens)
        this.#providerReservations.set(token, node);
      return node;
    }
    remove(node) {
      this.#nodes.delete(node);
      for (const token of new Set(Object.values(node.provides))) {
        if (this.#providerReservations.get(token) === node) {
          this.#providerReservations.delete(token);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function findDependencyCycle(nodes, providers) {
    const visiting = new Set;
    const visited = new Set;
    const nodeStack = [];
    const edgeStack = [];
    const visit = (node) => {
      visiting.add(node);
      nodeStack.push(node);
      for (const token of Object.values(node.requires)) {
        const provider = providers.get(token);
        if (!provider)
          continue;
        const edge = { consumer: node, provider, token };
        if (visiting.has(provider)) {
          const cycleStart = nodeStack.indexOf(provider);
          return [...edgeStack.slice(cycleStart), edge];
