        }
        if (visited.has(provider))
          continue;
        edgeStack.push(edge);
        const cycle = visit(provider);
        if (cycle)
          return cycle;
        edgeStack.pop();
      }
      nodeStack.pop();
      visiting.delete(node);
      visited.add(node);
      return;
    };
    for (const node of nodes) {
      if (visited.has(node))
        continue;
      const cycle = visit(node);
      if (cycle)
        return cycle;
    }
    return;
  }
  function formatDependencyCycle(cycle) {
    let path = `"${cycle[0].consumer.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}"`;
    for (const edge of cycle) {
      path += ` --[${edge.token.name}]--> "${edge.provider.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}"`;
    }
    return `Plugin dependency cycle: ${path}.`;
  }
  function createDependencyCycleDetails(cycle) {
    return Object.freeze({
      type: "dependency-cycle",
      path: Object.freeze(cycle.map((edge) => Object.freeze({
        plugin: edge.consumer.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>",
        serviceName: edge.token.name,
        provider: edge.provider.pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"
      })))
    });
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/provided-services.js
  function validateProvidedServices(pluginName, provides, result) {
    const bindingsByToken = new Map;
    for (const [binding, token] of Object.entries(provides)) {
      const duplicate = bindingsByToken.get(token);
      if (duplicate !== undefined) {
        throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" declares Provided Service Token "${token.name}" through both "${duplicate}" and "${binding}".`);
      }
      bindingsByToken.set(token, binding);
    }
    const declaredBindings = Object.keys(provides);
    if (!declaredBindings.length) {
      if (result !== undefined) {
        throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" returned a value without declaring a Provided Service.`);
      }
      return Object.freeze(Object.create(null));
    }
    const inspection = inspectPlainRecord(result, "invalid-prototype");
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-value") {
      throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" must return a Provided Service record.`);
    }
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-prototype") {
      throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" must return a plain Provided Service record.`);
    }
    if (inspection.status === "invalid-key") {
      throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" returned undeclared Provided Service binding "${String(inspection.key)}".`);
    }
    for (const binding of inspection.keys) {
      if (!Object.hasOwn(provides, binding)) {
        throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" returned undeclared Provided Service binding "${String(binding)}".`);
      }
    }
    const normalized = Object.create(null);
    for (const binding of declaredBindings) {
      if (!Object.hasOwn(inspection.record, binding)) {
        throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" did not return Provided Service binding "${binding}".`);
      }
      const value = inspection.record[binding];
      if (value == null) {
        throw new PluginHostError("CONTRACT_VIOLATION", `Plugin "${pluginName ?? "<anonymous>"}" returned ${String(value)} for Provided Service binding "${binding}".`);
      }
      normalized[binding] = value;
    }
    return Object.freeze(normalized);
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plugin-activation.js
  class PluginActivation {
    definition;
    config;
    installChild;
    publishService;
    diagnostics;
    controller = new AbortController;
    resources = [];
    #disposal;
    #ended = false;
    constructor(definition, config, installChild, publishService, diagnostics) {
      this.definition = definition;
      this.config = config;
      this.installChild = installChild;
      this.publishService = publishService;
      this.diagnostics = diagnostics;
    }
    async run(context) {
      const services = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(this.definition.requires ?? {}).map(([binding, token]) => [
        binding,
        context.get(getServiceName(token))
      ]));
      try {
        const result = await this.definition.setup({
          diagnostics: this.diagnostics,
          signal: this.controller.signal,
          services: Object.freeze(services),
          own: (dispose) => {
            this.#assertActive();
            this.resources.push(dispose);
          },
          install: (plugin, ...args) => {
            this.#assertActive();
            const child = this.installChild(plugin, ...args);
            this.resources.push(() => child.dispose());
            return child;
          }
        }, this.config);
        return validateProvidedServices(this.definition.name, this.definition.provides, result);
      } catch (error) {
        try {
          await this.dispose("setup-failed");
        } catch {}
        throw error;
      }
    }
    dispose(reason = "installation-disposed") {
      if (!this.#disposal) {
        this.#ended = true;
        this.controller.abort();
        this.#disposal = (async () => {
          try {
            const errors = [];
            for (const dispose of this.resources.splice(0).reverse()) {
              try {
                await dispose();
              } catch (error) {
                errors.push(error);
              }
            }
            if (errors.length) {
              throw new AggregateError(errors, "Plugin resource cleanup failed.");
            }
          } finally {
            this.diagnostics.emit({
              name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.activationEnded,
              level: "debug",
              attributes: { reason }
            });
          }
        })();
      }
      return this.#disposal;
    }
    publishProvidedServices(services) {
      return Object.entries(this.definition.provides ?? {}).map(([binding, token]) => this.publishService(getServiceName(token), services[binding]));
    }
    #assertActive() {
      if (this.#ended) {
        throw new PluginHostError("INSTALLATION_DISPOSED", "Plugin Activation has ended.");
      }
    }
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/plugin-installation.js
  class CordisPluginInstallation {
    definition;
    config;
    diagnostics;
    getMissing;
    installChild;
    publishService;
    reportDependencyCleanupError;
    onDisposed;
    #snapshot;
    #listeners = new Set;
    #waiters = new Set;
    #fiber;
    #currentActivation;
    #currentDeactivation;
    #cleanupErrors = [];
    #disposal;
    #disposeRequested = false;
    #failed = false;
    #terminalError;
    constructor(definition, config, diagnostics, missing, getMissing, installChild, publishService, reportDependencyCleanupError, onDisposed) {
      this.definition = definition;
      this.config = config;
      this.diagnostics = diagnostics;
      this.getMissing = getMissing;
      this.installChild = installChild;
      this.publishService = publishService;
      this.reportDependencyCleanupError = reportDependencyCleanupError;
      this.onDisposed = onDisposed;
      this.#snapshot = createPendingSnapshot(missing);
      this.#emitStatusChanged("none", this.#snapshot);
    }
    attachFiber(fiber) {
      this.#fiber = fiber;
    }
    getSnapshot() {
      return this.#snapshot;
    }
    subscribe(listener) {
      this.#listeners.add(listener);
      return () => {
        this.#listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    }
    whenActive(signal) {
      if (this.#disposeRequested) {
        return Promise.reject(this.#disposedError());
      }
      if (this.#snapshot.status === "active")
        return Promise.resolve();
      if (this.#snapshot.status === "failed") {
        return Promise.reject(this.#snapshot.error);
      }
      if (signal?.aborted)
        return Promise.reject(getAbortReason(signal));
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const waiter = {
          resolve,
          reject,
          signal,
          onAbort: signal ? () => {
            this.#waiters.delete(waiter);
            reject(getAbortReason(signal));
          } : undefined
        };
        if (waiter.onAbort)
          signal.addEventListener("abort", waiter.onAbort, { once: true });
        this.#waiters.add(waiter);
      });
    }
    async activate(context) {
      if (this.#failed)
        throw this.#terminalError;
      const activationDiagnostics = this.diagnostics.createActivation();
      activationDiagnostics.emit({
        name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.activationStarted,
        level: "debug"
      });
      const activation = new PluginActivation(this.definition, this.config, this.installChild, this.publishService, activationDiagnostics);
      this.#currentActivation = activation;
      const requiredServiceNames = new Set(Object.values(this.definition.requires ?? {}).map(getServiceName));
      let disappearingServiceName;
      context.on("internal/service", (name) => {
        if (requiredServiceNames.has(name) && context.get(name) === undefined) {
          disappearingServiceName ??= name;
          activation.controller.abort();
        }
      });
      let providedServices;
      try {
        providedServices = await activation.run(context);
      } catch (error) {
        if (activation.controller.signal.aborted && (this.#disposeRequested || this.getMissing().length > 0) && isCancellationError(error, activation.controller.signal)) {
          try {
            await activation.dispose(this.#disposeRequested ? "installation-disposed" : "dependency-lost");
          } catch (cleanupError) {
            if (!this.#disposeRequested) {
              this.#failDependencyCleanup(activation, disappearingServiceName, cleanupError);
              return async () => {};
            }
          }
          if (!this.#disposeRequested) {
            this.#setSnapshot(createPendingSnapshot(this.getMissing()));
          }
          return async () => {};
        }
        this.#fail(error, "setup");
        throw error;
      }
      if (activation.controller.signal.aborted || this.#disposeRequested) {
        try {
          await activation.dispose(this.#disposeRequested ? "installation-disposed" : "dependency-lost");
        } catch (cleanupError) {
          if (!this.#disposeRequested) {
            this.#failDependencyCleanup(activation, disappearingServiceName, cleanupError);
            return async () => {};
          }
        }
        if (!this.#disposeRequested) {
          this.#setSnapshot(createPendingSnapshot(this.getMissing()));
        }
        return async () => {};
      }
      const withdrawals = activation.publishProvidedServices(providedServices);
      Promise.resolve().then(() => Promise.resolve(this.#fiber)).then(() => {
        if (this.#currentActivation === activation && !activation.controller.signal.aborted && !this.#disposeRequested && !this.#failed) {
          this.#setSnapshot(Object.freeze({ status: "active" }));
          this.#resolveWaiters();
        }
      }).catch((error) => {
        if (this.#disposeRequested || this.#failed)
          return;
        this.#fail(error, "activation");
      });
      let deactivation;
      const deactivate = () => {
        deactivation ??= (async () => {
          activation.controller.abort();
          const dependencyDriven = !this.#disposeRequested && (disappearingServiceName !== undefined || this.getMissing().length > 0);
          if (dependencyDriven && !this.#failed) {
            this.#setSnapshot(createPendingSnapshot(this.getMissing()));
          }
          const errors = [];
          for (const withdraw of withdrawals.reverse()) {
            try {
              await withdraw();
            } catch (error) {
              collectCleanupError(errors, error);
            }
          }
          try {
            await activation.dispose(this.#disposeRequested ? "installation-disposed" : dependencyDriven ? "dependency-lost" : "setup-completed");
          } catch (error) {
            collectCleanupError(errors, error);
          }
          if (this.#currentActivation === activation) {
            this.#currentActivation = undefined;
            this.#currentDeactivation = undefined;
          }
          if (!this.#disposeRequested && !this.#failed && !dependencyDriven) {
            this.#setSnapshot(createPendingSnapshot(this.getMissing()));
          }
          if (errors.length) {
            const error = new AggregateError(errors, "Plugin resource cleanup failed.");
            if (!this.#disposeRequested && dependencyDriven) {
              this.#failDependencyCleanup(activation, disappearingServiceName, error);
              return;
            }
            this.#cleanupErrors.push(...errors);
            if (!this.#disposeRequested) {
              this.#fail(error, "cleanup");
              throw error;
            }
          }
        })();
        return deactivation;
      };
      this.#currentDeactivation = deactivate;
      return deactivate;
    }
    dispose() {
      if (!this.#disposal) {
        let completeDisposal;
        let failDisposal;
        this.#disposal = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          completeDisposal = resolve;
          failDisposal = reject;
        });
        this.#disposeRequested = true;
        this.#rejectWaiters(this.#disposedError());
        this.#currentActivation?.controller.abort();
        const deactivation = this.#currentDeactivation?.();
        if (deactivation) {
          Promise.resolve(deactivation).catch(() => {});
        }
        Promise.resolve().then(() => this.#fiber.dispose()).then(async () => {
          try {
            await this.#currentActivation?.dispose("installation-disposed");
          } catch (error) {
            collectCleanupError(this.#cleanupErrors, error);
          }
          if (this.#cleanupErrors.length) {
            throw new AggregateError(this.#cleanupErrors, "Plugin resource cleanup failed.");
          }
        }).then(() => {
          this.onDisposed();
          this.#setSnapshot(Object.freeze({ status: "disposed" }));
        }, (error) => {
          this.onDisposed();
          this.#setSnapshot(Object.freeze({ status: "disposed" }));
          const diagnosticError = this.diagnostics.findUndiagnosedError(error);
          if (diagnosticError.found) {
            this.diagnostics.diagnostics.emit({
              name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.installationDisposeFailed,
              level: "error",
              attributes: { phase: "cleanup" },
              error: diagnosticError.error
            });
          }
          throw error;
        }).then(completeDisposal, failDisposal);
      }
      return this.#disposal;
    }
    #setSnapshot(snapshot, failurePhase) {
      if (this.#snapshot === snapshot)
        return;
      const previousStatus = this.#snapshot.status;
      this.#snapshot = snapshot;
      this.#emitStatusChanged(previousStatus, snapshot, failurePhase);
      for (const listener of this.#listeners) {
        try {
          listener();
        } catch (error) {
          this.diagnostics.diagnostics.reportFault(error, {
            name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.installationSubscriberFault,
            attributes: { status: snapshot.status }
          });
        }
      }
    }
    #emitStatusChanged(from, snapshot, failurePhase) {
      this.diagnostics.diagnostics.emit({
        name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.installationStatusChanged,
        level: snapshot.status === "failed" ? "error" : "debug",
        attributes: {
          from,
          to: snapshot.status,
          ...snapshot.status === "pending" ? { missingServiceNames: snapshot.missing.map((service) => service.name).sort() } : {},
          ...snapshot.status === "failed" && failurePhase ? { phase: failurePhase } : {}
        },
        ...snapshot.status === "failed" ? { error: snapshot.error } : {}
      });
    }
    #resolveWaiters() {
      for (const waiter of this.#waiters) {
        if (waiter.onAbort)
          waiter.signal.removeEventListener("abort", waiter.onAbort);
        waiter.resolve();
      }
      this.#waiters.clear();
    }
    #rejectWaiters(error) {
      for (const waiter of this.#waiters) {
        if (waiter.onAbort)
          waiter.signal.removeEventListener("abort", waiter.onAbort);
        waiter.reject(error);
      }
      this.#waiters.clear();
    }
    #failDependencyCleanup(activation, disappearingServiceName, error) {
      const aggregate = error instanceof AggregateError ? error : new AggregateError([error], "Plugin resource cleanup failed.");
      if (this.#currentActivation === activation)
        this.#currentActivation = undefined;
      this.#fail(aggregate, "cleanup");
      const missingService = this.getMissing()[0];
      const serviceName = disappearingServiceName ?? (missingService ? getServiceName(missingService) : undefined);
      if (serviceName)
        this.reportDependencyCleanupError(serviceName, aggregate);
    }
    #fail(error, phase) {
      this.#failed = true;
      this.#terminalError = error;
      this.#setSnapshot(Object.freeze({ status: "failed", error }), phase);
      this.#rejectWaiters(error);
    }
    #disposedError() {
      return new PluginHostError("INSTALLATION_DISPOSED", "Plugin Installation has been disposed.");
    }
  }
  function createPendingSnapshot(missing) {
    return Object.freeze({
      status: "pending",
      missing: Object.freeze([...missing])
    });
  }
  function getAbortReason(signal) {
    if (signal.reason !== undefined)
      return signal.reason;
    const error = new Error("The operation was aborted.");
    error.name = "AbortError";
    return error;
  }
  function isCancellationError(error, signal) {
    if (error === signal.reason)
      return true;
    return error instanceof Error && error.name === "AbortError";
  }

  // packages/runtime-cordis/dist/cordis-plugin-host.js
  var cordisPlugins = new WeakMap;

  class CordisPluginHost {
    #context = new Context;
    #diagnostics;
    #dependencyCleanupErrors = new Map;
    #publishService = (name, value) => {
      const withdraw = this.#context.provide(name, value);
      let disposal;
      return () => {
        disposal ??= (async () => {
          const errors = [];
          try {
            await withdraw();
          } catch (error) {
            collectCleanupError(errors, error);
          }
          const dependencyErrors = this.#dependencyCleanupErrors.get(name);
          this.#dependencyCleanupErrors.delete(name);
          if (dependencyErrors)
            errors.push(...dependencyErrors);
          if (errors.length) {
            throw new AggregateError(errors, "Required Service cleanup failed.");
          }
        })();
        return disposal;
      };
    };
    #reportDependencyCleanupError = (serviceName, error) => {
      const errors = this.#dependencyCleanupErrors.get(serviceName) ?? [];
      collectCleanupError(errors, error);
      this.#dependencyCleanupErrors.set(serviceName, errors);
    };
    #rootInstallations = new Set;
    #graph = new PluginGraph;
    #closed = false;
    #disposal;
    constructor(options = {}) {
      this.#diagnostics = new HostDiagnostics(options.diagnostics);
      this.#diagnostics.emit({
        name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.hostCreated,
        level: "info"
      });
    }
    install(plugin, ...args) {
      return this.#install(plugin, args, true);
    }
    #install(plugin, args, isRoot) {
      if (this.#closed) {
        throw new PluginHostError("HOST_DISPOSED", "Plugin Host is disposing or has been disposed.");
      }
      const internal = getInternalPlugin(plugin);
      if (!internal) {
        throw new PluginHostError("INVALID_DEFINITION", "Plugin must be created with definePlugin().");
      }
      const definition = internal.definition;
      const requires = definition.requires ?? {};
      const provides = definition.provides ?? {};
      const node = this.#graph.register(plugin.name, requires, provides);
      try {
        const getMissing = () => Object.values(requires).filter((token) => this.#context.get(getServiceName(token)) === undefined);
        let installation;
        installation = new CordisPluginInstallation(definition, args[0], this.#diagnostics.createInstallation(plugin.name), getMissing(), getMissing, (childPlugin, ...childArgs) => this.#install(childPlugin, childArgs, false), this.#publishService, this.#reportDependencyCleanupError, () => {
          this.#rootInstallations.delete(installation);
          this.#graph.remove(node);
        });
        const fiber = this.#context.plugin(getCordisPlugin(internal), installation);
        installation.attachFiber(fiber);
        if (isRoot)
          this.#rootInstallations.add(installation);
        return installation;
      } catch (error) {
        this.#graph.remove(node);
        throw error;
      }
    }
    dispose() {
      if (!this.#disposal) {
        let completeDisposal;
        let failDisposal;
        this.#disposal = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          completeDisposal = resolve;
          failDisposal = reject;
        });
        this.#closed = true;
        this.#diagnostics.emit({
          name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.hostDisposing,
          level: "debug"
        });
        (async () => {
          const results = await Promise.allSettled([...this.#rootInstallations].map((installation) => installation.dispose()));
          const errors = [];
          for (const result of results) {
            if (result.status === "fulfilled")
              continue;
            collectCleanupError(errors, result.reason);
          }
          if (errors.length) {
            throw new AggregateError(errors, "Plugin Host cleanup failed.");
          }
          this.#diagnostics.emit({
            name: runtimeDiagnosticEvents.hostDisposed,
            level: "info"
          });
        })().then(completeDisposal, failDisposal);
      }
      return this.#disposal;
    }
  }
  function createPluginHost(options) {
    return new CordisPluginHost(options);
  }
  function getCordisPlugin(internal) {
    let plugin = cordisPlugins.get(internal);
    if (!plugin) {
      plugin = async (context, installation) => installation.activate(context);
      plugin.inject = Object.values(internal.definition.requires ?? {}).map(getServiceName);
      cordisPlugins.set(internal, plugin);
    }
    return plugin;
  }
  // packages/plugin-command/dist/command-plugin.js
  var commandService = defineService("command");
  var commandPlugin = definePlugin({
    name: "@nodebraid/plugin-command",
    provides: { commands: commandService },
    setup(context) {
      const registrations = new Map;
      const commandsById = new Map;
      let disposed = false;
      const assertActive = () => {
        if (disposed) {
          throw new CommandError("SERVICE_DISPOSED", "Command Service Activation has been disposed.");
        }
      };
      const getActiveRegistration = (command) => {
        assertActive();
        const commandId = getCommandId(command);
        const registration = registrations.get(command);
        if (!registration || registration.disposePromise) {
          throw new CommandError("COMMAND_NOT_FOUND", `Command "${commandId}" is not registered.`, Object.freeze({ commandId }));
        }
        return registration;
      };
      const disposeRegistration = (registration) => {
        if (registration.disposePromise)
          return registration.disposePromise;
        let completeDisposal;
        registration.disposePromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
          completeDisposal = resolve;
        });
        const executions = Array.from(registration.executions);
        for (const execution of executions)
          execution.controller.abort();
        Promise.all(executions.map((execution) => execution.settled)).then(() => {
          if (registrations.get(registration.command) === registration) {
            registrations.delete(registration.command);
          }
          if (commandsById.get(registration.commandId) === registration) {
            commandsById.delete(registration.commandId);
          }
          completeDisposal();
        });
        return registration.disposePromise;
      };
      const service = Object.freeze({
        register(command, handler) {
          assertActive();
          const commandId = getCommandId(command);
          if (registrations.has(command) || commandsById.has(commandId)) {
            throw new CommandError("COMMAND_ALREADY_REGISTERED", `Command "${commandId}" is already registered.`, Object.freeze({ commandId }));
          }
          const registration = {
            command,
            commandId,
            handler,
            executions: new Set
          };
          registrations.set(registration.command, registration);
          commandsById.set(commandId, registration);
          return Object.freeze({
            dispose() {
              return disposeRegistration(registration);
            }
          });
        },
        async execute(command, input, options) {
          getActiveRegistration(command);
          const callerSignal = options?.signal;
          const activeRegistration = getActiveRegistration(command);
          const commandId = activeRegistration.commandId;
          const controller = new AbortController;
          const abort = () => controller.abort(callerSignal?.reason);
          callerSignal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
          if (callerSignal?.aborted)
            abort();
          let settleExecution;
          const settled = new Promise((resolve) => {
            settleExecution = resolve;
          });
          const execution = { controller, settled };
          activeRegistration.executions.add(execution);
          const executionContext = Object.freeze({
            commandId,
            signal: controller.signal
          });
          let result;
          try {
            result = Promise.resolve(activeRegistration.handler(input, executionContext));
          } catch (error) {
            result = Promise.reject(error);
          }
          result.then(() => finishExecution(), () => finishExecution());
          return result;
          function finishExecution() {
            activeRegistration.executions.delete(execution);
            callerSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
            settleExecution();
          }
        }
      });
      context.own(async () => {
        disposed = true;
        const remainingRegistrations = Array.from(registrations.values());
        await Promise.all(remainingRegistrations.map((registration) => disposeRegistration(registration)));
      });
      return { commands: service };
    }
  });
  // packages/plugin-kernel/dist/diagnostic-events.js
  var kernelPluginDiagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    observerFault: "nodebraid.plugin.kernel.observer.fault"
  });

  // packages/plugin-kernel/dist/kernel-plugin-error.js
  class KernelPluginError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "KernelPluginError";
    constructor(code, message, options) {
      super("plugin.kernel", code, message, { cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

  // packages/plugin-kernel/dist/kernel-plugin.js
  var kernelService = defineService("kernel");
  var kernelPlugin = definePlugin({
    name: "@nodebraid/plugin-kernel",
    provides: { kernel: kernelService },
    setup(context) {
      const kernel = createCanvasKernel();
      const observers = new Set;
      const commitQueue = [];
      let dispatching = false;
      let disposed = false;
      const assertActive = () => {
        if (disposed) {
          throw new KernelPluginError("SERVICE_DISPOSED", "Kernel Service Activation has been disposed.");
        }
      };
      const dispatch = (commit) => {
        commitQueue.push(commit);
        if (dispatching)
          return;
        dispatching = true;
        try {
          for (const queuedCommit of commitQueue) {
            const currentObservers = Array.from(observers);
            for (const observer of currentObservers) {
              try {
                observer(queuedCommit);
              } catch (error) {
                context.diagnostics.reportFault(error, {
                  name: kernelPluginDiagnosticEvents.observerFault,
                  attributes: { revision: queuedCommit.changeSet.revision }
                });
              }
            }
          }
        } finally {
          commitQueue.length = 0;
          dispatching = false;
        }
      };
      const service = Object.freeze({
        read() {
          assertActive();
          return kernel.read();
        },
        transact(callback, metadata) {
          assertActive();
          const commit = kernel.transact(callback, metadata);
          if (commit)
            dispatch(commit);
          return commit;
        },
        observeCommits(observer) {
          assertActive();
          observers.add(observer);
          return () => observers.delete(observer);
        }
      });
      context.own(() => {
        disposed = true;
        observers.clear();
        commitQueue.length = 0;
      });
      return { kernel: service };
    }
  });
  // packages/plugin-port/dist/diagnostic-events.js
  var portDiagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    subscriberFault: "nodebraid.plugin.port.subscriber.fault"
  });

  // packages/plugin-port/dist/port-error.js
  class PortError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "PortError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("plugin.port", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

  // packages/plugin-port/dist/port-runtime.js
  function createPortRuntime(diagnostics) {
    const entries = new Map;
    const listeners = new Set;
    let generation = 0;
    let disposed = false;
    const assertActive = () => {
      if (disposed)
        throw new PortError("SERVICE_DISPOSED", "Port Service has been disposed.");
    };
    const notify = () => {
      for (const listener of Array.from(listeners)) {
        try {
          listener();
        } catch (error) {
          diagnostics.reportFault(error, { name: portDiagnosticEvents.subscriberFault });
        }
      }
    };
    const service = Object.freeze({
      register(value) {
        assertActive();
        const contribution = acceptContribution(value);
        if (entries.has(contribution.id)) {
          throw new PortError("CONTRIBUTION_ALREADY_REGISTERED", "Port Contribution ID is already registered.", {
            contributionId: contribution.id
          });
        }
        const entry = { contribution, disposed: false };
        entries.set(contribution.id, entry);
        generation += 1;
        notify();
        return Object.freeze({
          dispose() {
            if (entry.disposed)
              return;
            entry.disposed = true;
            if (entries.get(contribution.id) !== entry)
              return;
            entries.delete(contribution.id);
            generation += 1;
            if (!disposed)
              notify();
          }
        });
      },
      snapshot(document2) {
        assertActive();
        if (!isCanvasSnapshot(document2)) {
          throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port Snapshot requires a committed Canvas Snapshot.");
        }
        const contributions = Array.from(entries.values(), (entry) => entry.contribution).sort((left, right) => compareIds2(left.id, right.id));
        const nodes = [];
        for (const node of document2.nodes) {
          const ports = resolveNodePorts(node, contributions);
          if (ports.length > 0)
            nodes.push(Object.freeze({ nodeId: node.id, ports }));
        }
        return Object.freeze({
          documentRevision: document2.revision,
          generation,
          nodes: Object.freeze(nodes)
        });
      },
      isConnectionTargetDefined(node, target) {
        assertActive();
        if (node.id !== target.nodeId)
          return false;
        const contributions = Array.from(entries.values(), (entry) => entry.contribution).sort((left, right) => compareIds2(left.id, right.id));
        const definitions = resolveNodePorts(node, contributions);
        if (target.portId === undefined)
          return definitions.length === 0;
        return definitions.find((definition) => definition.id === target.portId)?.role === target.role;
      },
      subscribe(listener) {
        assertActive();
        if (typeof listener !== "function") {
          throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port subscriber must be a function.");
        }
        listeners.add(listener);
        let active = true;
        return () => {
          if (!active)
            return;
          active = false;
          listeners.delete(listener);
        };
      }
    });
    return {
      service,
      dispose() {
        if (disposed)
          return;
        disposed = true;
        listeners.clear();
        entries.clear();
        generation += 1;
      }
    };
  }
  function acceptContribution(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port Contribution is malformed.");
    }
    const id = value.id;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0) {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port Contribution is malformed.");
    }
    const definePorts = value.definePorts;
    if (typeof definePorts !== "function") {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port Contribution is malformed.");
    }
    for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
      if (key === "id" || key === "definePorts")
        continue;
      throw new PortError("INVALID_CONTRIBUTION", "Port Contribution contains an unknown field.");
    }
    return Object.freeze({ id, definePorts });
  }
  function resolveNodePorts(node, contributions) {
    const definitions = new Map;
    for (const contribution of contributions) {
      const received = contribution.definePorts(node);
      if (!Array.isArray(received)) {
        throw new PortError("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION", "Port Contribution must return an array.", {
          contributionId: contribution.id
        });
      }
      for (const value of received) {
        const definition = acceptPortDefinition(value, contribution.id);
        if (definitions.has(definition.id)) {
          throw new PortError("PORT_ALREADY_DEFINED", "Port ID is already defined for this Node.", {
            contributionId: contribution.id,
            portId: definition.id
          });
        }
        definitions.set(definition.id, definition);
      }
    }
    return Object.freeze(Array.from(definitions.values()).sort((left, right) => compareIds2(left.id, right.id)));
  }
  function acceptPortDefinition(value, contributionId) {
    if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION", "Port Definition is malformed.", { contributionId });
    }
    const id = Reflect.get(value, "id");
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0) {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION", "Port Definition is malformed.", { contributionId });
    }
    const role = Reflect.get(value, "role");
    if (role !== "source" && role !== "target") {
      throw new PortError("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION", "Port Definition is malformed.", { contributionId });
    }
    for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
      if (key === "id" || key === "role")
        continue;
      throw new PortError("INVALID_PORT_DEFINITION", "Port Definition contains an unknown field.", {
        contributionId
      });
    }
    return Object.freeze({ id, role });
  }
  function isCanvasSnapshot(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && Number.isInteger(value.revision) && value.revision >= 0 && Array.isArray(value.nodes) && Array.isArray(value.edges);
  }
  function compareIds2(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
  }

  // packages/plugin-port/dist/port-plugin.js
  var portService = defineService("ports");
  var portPlugin = definePlugin({
    name: "@nodebraid/plugin-port",
    provides: { ports: portService },
    setup(context) {
      const runtime = createPortRuntime(context.diagnostics);
      context.own(runtime.dispose);
      return { ports: runtime.service };
    }
  });
  function createPortContributionPlugin(contribution) {
    return definePlugin({
      name: "@nodebraid/plugin-port/contribution",
      requires: { ports: portService },
      setup(context) {
        const registration = context.services.ports.register(contribution);
        context.own(() => registration.dispose());
      }
    });
  }
  // packages/plugin-session/dist/diagnostic-events.js
  var sessionDiagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    subscriberFault: "nodebraid.plugin.session.subscriber.fault"
  });

  // packages/plugin-session/dist/session-error.js
  class SessionError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "SessionError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("plugin.session", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

  // packages/plugin-session/dist/session-plugin.js
  var sessionService = defineService("session");
  var sessionPlugin = definePlugin({
    name: "@nodebraid/plugin-session",
    requires: { kernel: kernelService },
    provides: { session: sessionService },
    setup(context) {
      context.services.kernel;
      const emptySelection = createSelectionSnapshot({ nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] });
      const defaultViewport = Object.freeze({ x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 });
      let snapshot = createSessionSnapshot(emptySelection, defaultViewport);
      const subscriptions = [];
      const transitions = [];
      let transitioning = false;
      let disposed = false;
      const assertActive = () => {
        if (disposed) {
          throw new SessionError("SERVICE_DISPOSED", "Session Service Activation has been disposed.");
        }
      };
      const enqueueTransition = (transition) => {
        transitions.push(transition);
        if (transitioning)
          return;
        transitioning = true;
        try {
          for (const queuedTransition of transitions) {
            const nextSnapshot = queuedTransition(snapshot);
            if (!nextSnapshot)
              continue;
            snapshot = nextSnapshot;
            const currentListeners = subscriptions.filter((registration) => registration.active).map(({ listener }) => listener);
            for (const listener of currentListeners) {
              try {
                listener();
              } catch (error) {
                context.diagnostics.reportFault(error, {
                  name: sessionDiagnosticEvents.subscriberFault
                });
              }
            }
          }
        } finally {
          transitions.length = 0;
          transitioning = false;
        }
      };
      const publishSelection = (selection) => {
        enqueueTransition((current) => selectionsEqual(current.selection, selection) ? null : createSessionSnapshot(selection, current.viewport));
      };
      const publishViewport = (viewport) => {
        enqueueTransition((current) => viewportsEqual2(current.viewport, viewport) ? null : createSessionSnapshot(current.selection, viewport));
      };
      const service = Object.freeze({
        getSnapshot() {
          assertActive();
          return snapshot;
        },
        subscribe(listener) {
          assertActive();
          if (typeof listener !== "function") {
            throw new SessionError("INVALID_SUBSCRIBER", "Session subscriber must be a function.", Object.freeze({ receivedType: describeReceivedType(listener) }));
          }
          const registration = { listener, active: true };
          subscriptions.push(registration);
          return () => {
            if (!registration.active)
              return;
            registration.active = false;
            const index = subscriptions.indexOf(registration);
            if (index >= 0)
              subscriptions.splice(index, 1);
          };
        },
        setSelection(selection) {
          assertActive();
          const captured = typeof selection === "object" && selection !== null ? { nodeIds: captureSelectionValues(selection.nodeIds), edgeIds: captureSelectionValues(selection.edgeIds) } : selection;
          validateSelectionInput(captured);
          const nextSelection = createSelectionSnapshot(captured);
          const view = context.services.kernel.read();
          const missingNodeIds = Object.freeze(nextSelection.nodeIds.filter((id) => !view.query.getNode(id)));
          const missingEdgeIds = Object.freeze(nextSelection.edgeIds.filter((id) => !view.query.getEdge(id)));
          if (missingNodeIds.length > 0 || missingEdgeIds.length > 0) {
            throw new SessionError("SELECTION_ENTITY_NOT_FOUND", "Selection contains entities that do not exist in the current Canvas View.", Object.freeze({ missingNodeIds, missingEdgeIds }));
          }
          publishSelection(nextSelection);
        },
        clearSelection() {
          assertActive();
          publishSelection(emptySelection);
        },
        setViewport(viewport) {
          assertActive();
          const captured = typeof viewport === "object" && viewport !== null ? { x: viewport.x, y: viewport.y, zoom: viewport.zoom } : viewport;
          const issues = collectViewportIssues(captured);
          if (issues.length > 0) {
            throw new SessionError("INVALID_VIEWPORT", "Viewport requires finite x and y values and a finite zoom greater than zero.", Object.freeze({ issues }));
          }
          publishViewport(Object.freeze({
            x: normalizeZero(captured.x),
            y: normalizeZero(captured.y),
            zoom: captured.zoom
          }));
        }
      });
      const stopObservingCommits = context.services.kernel.observeCommits((commit) => {
        enqueueTransition((current) => {
          const selection = current.selection;
          const nodeIds = selection.nodeIds.filter((id) => commit.after.query.getNode(id));
          const edgeIds = selection.edgeIds.filter((id) => commit.after.query.getEdge(id));
          if (nodeIds.length === selection.nodeIds.length && edgeIds.length === selection.edgeIds.length)
            return null;
          return createSessionSnapshot(createSelectionSnapshot({ nodeIds, edgeIds }), current.viewport);
        });
      });
      context.own(stopObservingCommits);
      context.own(() => {
        disposed = true;
        for (const registration of subscriptions)
          registration.active = false;
        subscriptions.length = 0;
        transitions.length = 0;
      });
      return { session: service };
    }
  });
  function captureSelectionValues(value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(value))
      return value;
    const captured = [];
    const length = +value.length;
    for (const index of Array.prototype.keys.call({ length }))
      captured.push(value[index]);
    return captured;
  }
  function createSelectionSnapshot(selection) {
    const nodeIds = Object.freeze([...new Set(selection.nodeIds)].sort(compareIds3));
    const edgeIds = Object.freeze([...new Set(selection.edgeIds)].sort(compareIds3));
    return Object.freeze({ nodeIds, edgeIds });
  }
  function createSessionSnapshot(selection, viewport) {
    return Object.freeze({ selection, viewport });
  }
  function selectionsEqual(left, right) {
    return arraysEqual(left.nodeIds, right.nodeIds) && arraysEqual(left.edgeIds, right.edgeIds);
  }
  function viewportsEqual2(left, right) {
    return left.x === right.x && left.y === right.y && left.zoom === right.zoom;
  }
  function arraysEqual(left, right) {
    return left.length === right.length && left.every((value, index) => value === right[index]);
  }
  function compareIds3(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
  }
  function normalizeZero(value) {
    return Object.is(value, -0) ? 0 : value;
  }
  function collectViewportIssues(viewport) {
    if (typeof viewport !== "object" || viewport === null) {
      const issue = Object.freeze({
        field: "viewport",
        code: "EXPECTED_OBJECT",
        receivedType: describeReceivedType(viewport)
      });
      return Object.freeze([issue]);
    }
    const issues = [];
    const x = Reflect.get(viewport, "x");
    const y = Reflect.get(viewport, "y");
    const zoom = Reflect.get(viewport, "zoom");
    if (typeof x !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(x))
      issues.push(describeInvalidViewportNumber("x", x));
    if (typeof y !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(y))
      issues.push(describeInvalidViewportNumber("y", y));
    if (typeof zoom !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(zoom) || zoom <= 0) {
      issues.push(describeInvalidViewportNumber("zoom", zoom));
    }
    return Object.freeze(issues);
  }
  function describeInvalidViewportNumber(field, value) {
    const code = field === "zoom" ? "EXPECTED_POSITIVE_NUMBER" : "EXPECTED_FINITE_NUMBER";
    if (typeof value !== "number") {
      return Object.freeze({ field, code, receivedType: describeReceivedType(value) });
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
      return Object.freeze({ field, code, receivedNumber: describeNonFiniteNumber(value) });
    }
    return Object.freeze({ field, code, value });
  }
  function describeReceivedType(value) {
    return value === null ? "null" : typeof value;
  }
  function validateSelectionInput(selection) {
    const issues = [];
    if (typeof selection !== "object" || selection === null) {
      issues.push(Object.freeze({
        field: "selection",
        code: "EXPECTED_OBJECT",
        receivedType: describeReceivedType(selection)
      }));
    } else {
      collectSelectionFieldIssues("nodeIds", Reflect.get(selection, "nodeIds"), issues);
      collectSelectionFieldIssues("edgeIds", Reflect.get(selection, "edgeIds"), issues);
    }
    if (issues.length > 0) {
      throw new SessionError("INVALID_SELECTION", "Selection requires NodeId and EdgeId arrays containing non-empty string identifiers.", Object.freeze({ issues: Object.freeze(issues) }));
    }
  }
  function collectSelectionFieldIssues(field, value, issues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
      issues.push(Object.freeze({ field, code: "EXPECTED_ARRAY", receivedType: describeReceivedType(value) }));
      return;
    }
    for (let index = 0;index < value.length; index += 1) {
      const id = value[index];
      if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0) {
        issues.push(Object.freeze({
          field,
          code: "INVALID_ID",
          index,
          ...typeof id === "string" ? { value: id } : { receivedType: describeReceivedType(id) }
        }));
      }
    }
  }
  // packages/plugin-renderer/dist/diagnostic-events.js
  var rendererDiagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    inputListenerFault: "nodebraid.plugin.renderer.input-listener.fault",
    syncFault: "nodebraid.plugin.renderer.sync.fault"
  });

  // packages/plugin-renderer/dist/renderer-plugin-error.js
  class RendererPluginError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "RendererPluginError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}, options) {
      super("plugin.renderer", code, message, { details, cause: options?.cause });
    }
  }

  // packages/plugin-renderer/dist/renderer-plugin.js
  var rendererService = defineService("renderer");
  function createRendererPlugin(factory) {
    if (typeof factory !== "function")
      throw new TypeError("Renderer Factory must be a function.");
    return definePlugin({
      name: "@nodebraid/plugin-renderer",
      requires: { kernel: kernelService, ports: portService, session: sessionService },
      provides: { renderer: rendererService },
      async setup(context, config) {
        const kernel = context.services.kernel;
        const ports = context.services.ports;
        const session = context.services.session;
        const renderer = await factory(config);
        context.own(() => renderer.dispose());
        context.signal.throwIfAborted();
        let disposed = false;
        let syncFailure;
        let draining = false;
        let deliveredView;
        let deliveredSession;
        let resetThroughRevision;
        const pendingUpdates = [];
        const inputSubscriptions = new Set;
        let interactionBindingActive = false;
        let stopKernel = () => {
          return;
        };
        let stopSession = () => {
          return;
        };
        let stopPorts = () => {
          return;
        };
        const assertActive = () => {
          if (disposed) {
            throw new RendererPluginError("SERVICE_DISPOSED", "Renderer Service Activation has been disposed.");
          }
        };
        const assertOperational = () => {
          assertActive();
          if (syncFailure)
            throw syncFailure;
        };
        const reportSyncFault = (error) => {
          context.diagnostics.reportFault(error, { name: rendererDiagnosticEvents.syncFault });
        };
        const resetToCurrentState = (subsumesQueuedCommits = false) => {
          const view = kernel.read();
          const snapshot = session.getSnapshot();
          assertSessionResolvable(snapshot, view);
          renderer.updateDocument({ type: "reset", view, ports: ports.snapshot(view.snapshot) });
          renderer.updateSession(snapshot);
          deliveredView = view;
          deliveredSession = snapshot;
          resetThroughRevision = subsumesQueuedCommits ? view.snapshot.revision : undefined;
        };
        const deliverCommit = (commit) => {
          const currentView = deliveredView;
          if (!currentView)
            throw new Error("Renderer Document Baseline has not been established.");
          const expectedRevision = currentView.snapshot.revision;
          if (resetThroughRevision !== undefined) {
            if (commit.after.snapshot.revision <= resetThroughRevision)
              return;
            resetThroughRevision = undefined;
          }
          const receivedRevision = commit.before.snapshot.revision;
          if (receivedRevision !== expectedRevision) {
            throw new RendererError("DOCUMENT_OUT_OF_SYNC", "Renderer Commit is not contiguous with its Baseline.", {
              expectedRevision,
              receivedRevision
            });
          }
          renderer.updateDocument({ type: "commit", commit, ports: ports.snapshot(commit.after.snapshot) });
          deliveredView = commit.after;
        };
        const deliverSession = (snapshot) => {
          renderer.updateSession(snapshot);
          deliveredSession = snapshot;
        };
        const findSessionBeforeNextCommit = () => {
          const view = deliveredView;
          if (!view)
            return -1;
          for (let index = 1;index < pendingUpdates.length; index += 1) {
            const update = pendingUpdates[index];
            if (update.type === "ports")
              continue;
            if (update.type === "commit")
              return -1;
            if (isSessionResolvable(update.snapshot, view))
              return index;
          }
          return -1;
        };
        const findCommitForPendingSession = () => {
          const currentSession = deliveredSession;
          if (!currentSession)
            return -1;
          for (let index = 1;index < pendingUpdates.length; index += 1) {
            const update = pendingUpdates[index];
            if (update.type === "ports")
              continue;
            if (update.type !== "commit")
              continue;
            return isSessionResolvable(currentSession, update.commit.after) ? index : -1;
          }
          return -1;
        };
        const deliverNextUpdate = () => {
          const currentView = deliveredView;
          const currentSession = deliveredSession;
          const first = pendingUpdates[0];
          if (!first || !currentView || !currentSession)
            return false;
          if (first.type === "ports") {
            pendingUpdates.shift();
            resetToCurrentState(true);
            return true;
          }
          if (first.type === "session") {
            if (isSessionResolvable(first.snapshot, currentView)) {
              pendingUpdates.shift();
              deliverSession(first.snapshot);
              return true;
            }
            const commitIndex = findCommitForPendingSession();
            if (commitIndex < 0)
              return false;
            const [commitUpdate] = pendingUpdates.splice(commitIndex, 1);
            if (!commitUpdate || commitUpdate.type !== "commit")
              return false;
            deliverCommit(commitUpdate.commit);
            return true;
          }
          if (isSessionResolvable(currentSession, first.commit.after)) {
            pendingUpdates.shift();
            deliverCommit(first.commit);
            return true;
          }
          const sessionIndex = findSessionBeforeNextCommit();
          if (sessionIndex < 0)
            return false;
          const [sessionUpdate] = pendingUpdates.splice(sessionIndex, 1);
          if (!sessionUpdate || sessionUpdate.type !== "session")
            return false;
          deliverSession(sessionUpdate.snapshot);
          return true;
        };
        const drainUpdates = () => {
          if (draining || disposed || syncFailure)
            return;
          draining = true;
          try {
            while (deliverNextUpdate()) {}
          } catch (error) {
            pendingUpdates.length = 0;
            reportSyncFault(error);
            try {
              resetToCurrentState(true);
            } catch (recoveryError) {
              syncFailure = new RendererPluginError("SYNC_FAILED", "Renderer synchronization and its single full recovery both failed.", {}, {
                cause: new AggregateError([error, recoveryError], "Renderer synchronization and recovery both failed.")
              });
              reportSyncFault(syncFailure);
            }
          } finally {
            draining = false;
          }
        };
        const enqueueUpdate = (update) => {
          if (syncFailure)
            return;
          pendingUpdates.push(update);
          drainUpdates();
        };
        stopKernel = kernel.observeCommits((commit) => {
          if (!disposed)
            enqueueUpdate({ type: "commit", commit });
        });
        stopSession = session.subscribe(() => {
          if (!disposed)
            enqueueUpdate({ type: "session", snapshot: session.getSnapshot() });
        });
        stopPorts = ports.subscribe(() => {
          if (!disposed)
            enqueueUpdate({ type: "ports" });
        });
        context.own(() => {
          if (disposed)
            return;
          disposed = true;
          pendingUpdates.length = 0;
          const cleanupErrors = [];
          for (const stop of [stopPorts, stopSession, stopKernel, ...inputSubscriptions]) {
            try {
              stop();
            } catch (error) {
              cleanupErrors.push(error);
            }
          }
          inputSubscriptions.clear();
          if (cleanupErrors.length > 0) {
            throw new AggregateError(cleanupErrors, "Renderer Runtime subscription cleanup failed.");
          }
        });
        resetToCurrentState();
        const service = Object.freeze({
          bindInteractionProjection() {
            assertOperational();
            if (interactionBindingActive) {
              throw new RendererPluginError("INTERACTION_ALREADY_BOUND", "Renderer Service already has an Interaction Projection Binding.");
            }
            interactionBindingActive = true;
            let bindingDisposed = false;
            return Object.freeze({
              update(projection) {
                assertOperational();
                if (bindingDisposed) {
                  throw new RendererPluginError("INTERACTION_BINDING_DISPOSED", "Interaction Projection Binding has been disposed.");
                }
                renderer.updateInteraction(projection);
              },
              dispose() {
                if (bindingDisposed)
                  return;
                bindingDisposed = true;
                renderer.updateInteraction(null);
                interactionBindingActive = false;
              }
            });
          },
          subscribeInput(listener, decideDisposition) {
            assertOperational();
            if (typeof listener !== "function") {
              throw new RendererError("INVALID_INPUT_SUBSCRIBER", "Renderer Input listener must be a function.", {
                receivedType: describeReceivedType2(listener)
              });
            }
            let active = true;
            const stopRenderer = renderer.subscribeInput((input) => {
              if (!active || disposed || syncFailure)
                return;
              try {
                return listener(input);
              } catch (error) {
                context.diagnostics.reportFault(error, {
                  name: rendererDiagnosticEvents.inputListenerFault,
                  attributes: { inputType: input.type }
                });
              }
            }, decideDisposition === undefined ? undefined : (input) => {
              if (!active || disposed || syncFailure)
                return;
              return decideDisposition(input);
            });
            const stop = () => {
              if (!active)
                return;
              active = false;
              inputSubscriptions.delete(stop);
              stopRenderer();
            };
            inputSubscriptions.add(stop);
            return stop;
          },
          hitTest(point) {
            assertOperational();
            return renderer.hitTest(point);
          },
          capturePointer(pointerId) {
            assertOperational();
            renderer.capturePointer(pointerId);
          },
          releasePointer(pointerId) {
            assertOperational();
            renderer.releasePointer(pointerId);
          },
          focus() {
            assertOperational();
            renderer.focus();
          }
        });
        return { renderer: service };
      }
    });
  }
  function isSessionResolvable(snapshot, view) {
    return snapshot.selection.nodeIds.every((id) => view.query.getNode(id) !== undefined) && snapshot.selection.edgeIds.every((id) => view.query.getEdge(id) !== undefined);
  }
  function assertSessionResolvable(snapshot, view) {
    if (isSessionResolvable(snapshot, view))
      return;
    throw new RendererError("INVALID_SESSION_SNAPSHOT", "Renderer Session Snapshot references entities outside the current Document.", { documentRevision: view.snapshot.revision });
  }
  function describeReceivedType2(value) {
    return value === null ? "null" : typeof value;
  }
  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/interaction-error.js
  class InteractionError extends NodeBraidError {
    name = "InteractionError";
    constructor(code, message, details = {}) {
      super("interaction", code, message, { details });
    }
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/interaction-config.js
  var configFields = new Set(["dragThreshold", "wheelZoomSensitivity", "minZoom", "maxZoom", "connection"]);
  function resolveInteractionConfig(config) {
    if (config !== undefined) {
      if (config === null || typeof config !== "object" || Array.isArray(config)) {
        throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction config must be an object.");
      }
      for (const field of Object.keys(config)) {
        if (!configFields.has(field)) {
          throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", `Unknown Interaction config field: ${field}.`);
        }
      }
    }
    const resolved = {
      dragThreshold: config?.dragThreshold ?? 4,
      wheelZoomSensitivity: config?.wheelZoomSensitivity ?? 0.002,
      minZoom: config?.minZoom ?? 0.1,
      maxZoom: config?.maxZoom ?? 8,
      connection: config?.connection
    };
    if (!Number.isFinite(resolved.dragThreshold) || resolved.dragThreshold < 0) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction dragThreshold must be a finite non-negative number.");
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(resolved.wheelZoomSensitivity) || resolved.wheelZoomSensitivity <= 0) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction wheelZoomSensitivity must be a finite positive number.");
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(resolved.minZoom) || resolved.minZoom <= 0) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction minZoom must be a finite positive number.");
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(resolved.maxZoom) || resolved.maxZoom <= 0) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction maxZoom must be a finite positive number.");
    }
    if (resolved.minZoom > resolved.maxZoom) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction minZoom must not exceed maxZoom.");
    }
    if (resolved.connection !== undefined) {
      if (!isRecord6(resolved.connection)) {
        throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction connection config must be an object.");
      }
      for (const field of Reflect.ownKeys(resolved.connection)) {
        if (field !== "materializeEdge") {
          throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction connection config contains an unknown field.");
        }
      }
      const materializeEdge = resolved.connection.materializeEdge;
      if (typeof materializeEdge !== "function") {
        throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONFIG", "Interaction connection materializeEdge must be a function.");
      }
      resolved.connection = Object.freeze({ materializeEdge });
    }
    return Object.freeze(resolved);
  }
  function isRecord6(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
  }

  // packages/interaction-api/dist/world-rect.js
  function createWorldRect(start, end) {
    return Object.freeze({
      x: Math.min(start.x, end.x),
      y: Math.min(start.y, end.y),
      width: Math.abs(end.x - start.x),
      height: Math.abs(end.y - start.y)
    });
  }
  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/selection-transition.js
  function computeClickSelection(current, hit, additive) {
    if (hit.type === "canvas")
      return additive ? current : { nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] };
    if (hit.type === "edge") {
      if (!additive)
        return { nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [hit.edgeId] };
      const selected = current.edgeIds.includes(hit.edgeId);
      return {
        nodeIds: current.nodeIds,
        edgeIds: selected ? current.edgeIds.filter((edgeId) => edgeId !== hit.edgeId) : [...current.edgeIds, hit.edgeId]
      };
    }
    const nodeId = hit.nodeId;
    if (!additive)
      return { nodeIds: [nodeId], edgeIds: [] };
    const selected = current.nodeIds.includes(nodeId);
    return {
      nodeIds: selected ? current.nodeIds.filter((selectedId) => selectedId !== nodeId) : [...current.nodeIds, nodeId],
      edgeIds: current.edgeIds
    };
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/box-selection.js
  function computeBoxSelection(current, nodes, rect, additive) {
    const intersectingNodeIds = nodes.filter((node) => node.size && rectanglesIntersect(rect, { ...node.position, ...node.size })).map((node) => node.id).sort(compareIds4);
    if (!additive)
      return { nodeIds: intersectingNodeIds, edgeIds: [] };
    return {
      nodeIds: [...new Set([...current.nodeIds, ...intersectingNodeIds])].sort(compareIds4),
      edgeIds: current.edgeIds
    };
  }
  function compareIds4(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
  }
  function rectanglesIntersect(left, right) {
    return left.x <= right.x + right.width && left.x + left.width >= right.x && left.y <= right.y + right.height && left.y + left.height >= right.y;
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/canvas-value-validation.js
  function isCanvasNode(value) {
    return isRecord7(value) && isId(value.id) && typeof value.type === "string" && isPoint2(value.position) && (value.size === undefined || isRecord7(value.size) && typeof value.size.width === "number" && typeof value.size.height === "number") && (value.parentId === undefined || isId(value.parentId)) && "data" in value;
  }
  function isCanvasEdge(value) {
    return isRecord7(value) && isId(value.id) && typeof value.type === "string" && isEndpoint2(value.source) && isEndpoint2(value.target) && "data" in value;
  }
  function isEndpoint2(value) {
    return isRecord7(value) && isId(value.nodeId) && (value.portId === undefined || typeof value.portId === "string");
  }
  function isPoint2(value) {
    return isRecord7(value) && typeof value.x === "number" && typeof value.y === "number";
  }
  function isId(value) {
    return typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0;
  }
  function isRecord7(value) {
    return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/create-edge-command.js
  var createEdgeCommand = defineCommand("interaction.edge.create");
  function createEdgeHandler(kernel, ports) {
    return (input, execution) => {
      execution.signal.throwIfAborted();
      const { edge, source, target } = captureCreateEdgeInput(input);
      const commit = kernel.transact((transaction) => {
        if (source.role !== "source" || target.role !== "target")
          throwInvalidConnection();
        if (edge.source.nodeId !== source.nodeId || edge.target.nodeId !== target.nodeId || edge.source.portId !== source.portId || edge.target.portId !== target.portId || source.nodeId === target.nodeId)
          throwInvalidConnection();
        const sourceNode = transaction.query.getNode(source.nodeId), targetNode = transaction.query.getNode(target.nodeId);
        if (!sourceNode || !targetNode)
          throw new InteractionError("STALE_GESTURE", "Create Edge Command Endpoint Node disappeared.");
        if (!ports.isConnectionTargetDefined(sourceNode, source) || !ports.isConnectionTargetDefined(targetNode, target))
          throw new InteractionError("STALE_GESTURE", "Create Edge Command Port Definition changed.");
        if (transaction.query.getEdge(edge.id))
          throw new InteractionError("STALE_GESTURE", "Create Edge Command Edge ID is no longer available.");
        transaction.edges.add(edge);
      }, { origin: "interaction", commandId: execution.commandId });
      if (!commit)
        throw new Error("Create Edge Command must produce a Canvas Commit.");
      return commit;
    };
  }
  function captureCreateEdgeInput(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const edge = captureEdge(value.edge);
    const source = captureConnectionAnchor(value.source);
    const target = captureConnectionAnchor(value.target);
    return { edge, source, target };
  }
  function captureEdge(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const id = value.id;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const type = value.type;
    if (typeof type !== "string")
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const source = captureEndpoint(value.source), target = captureEndpoint(value.target);
    if (!("data" in value))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    return {
      id: edgeId(id),
      type,
      source,
      target,
      get data() {
        return value.data;
      }
    };
  }
  function captureEndpoint(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const id = value.nodeId;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const portId = value.portId;
    if (portId !== undefined && typeof portId !== "string")
      throwInvalidConnection();
    return { nodeId: nodeId(id), ...portId === undefined ? {} : { portId } };
  }
  function captureConnectionAnchor(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const id = value.nodeId;
    if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const portId = value.portId;
    if (portId !== undefined && (typeof portId !== "string" || portId.length === 0))
      throwInvalidConnection();
    const role = value.role;
    if (role !== "source" && role !== "target")
      throwInvalidConnection();
    return { nodeId: nodeId(id), ...portId === undefined ? {} : { portId }, role };
  }
  function throwInvalidConnection() {
    throw new InteractionError("INVALID_CONNECTION", "Create Edge Command input is invalid.");
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/delete-selection-command.js
  var deleteSelectionCommand = defineCommand("interaction.selection.delete");
  function createDeleteSelectionHandler(kernel) {
    return (input, execution) => {
      execution.signal.throwIfAborted();
      const { selection, evidence } = captureDeleteSelectionInput(input);
      const nodeIds = canonicalIds(selection.nodeIds);
      const selectedEdgeIds = canonicalIds(selection.edgeIds);
      if (nodeIds.length === 0 && selectedEdgeIds.length === 0)
        return null;
      const evidenceNodes = new Map(evidence.nodes.map((node) => [node.id, node]));
      const evidenceEdges = new Map(evidence.edges.map((edge) => [edge.id, edge]));
      const selectedNodes = nodeIds.map((id) => requireEvidenceNode(evidenceNodes, id));
      const deletionEdges = new Map;
      for (const edgeId of selectedEdgeIds) {
        const edge = evidenceEdges.get(edgeId);
        if (!edge)
          throwInvalidDelete("Delete Selection evidence does not contain a selected Edge.");
        deletionEdges.set(edge.id, edge);
      }
      for (const edge of evidence.edges) {
        if (nodeIds.includes(edge.source.nodeId) || nodeIds.includes(edge.target.nodeId)) {
          deletionEdges.set(edge.id, edge);
        }
      }
      const edges = [...deletionEdges.values()].sort(compareEntities);
      return kernel.transact((transaction) => {
        for (const node of selectedNodes) {
          if (transaction.query.getNode(node.id) !== node)
            throwStaleSelection();
          const currentIncidentIds = transaction.query.getIncidentEdges(node.id).map((edge) => edge.id);
          const evidenceIncidentIds = evidence.edges.filter((edge) => edge.source.nodeId === node.id || edge.target.nodeId === node.id).map((edge) => edge.id).sort(compareIds5);
          if (!arraysEqual2(currentIncidentIds, evidenceIncidentIds))
            throwStaleSelection();
        }
        for (const edge of edges) {
          if (transaction.query.getEdge(edge.id) !== edge)
            throwStaleSelection();
        }
        for (const edge of edges)
          transaction.edges.remove(edge.id);
        for (const node of selectedNodes)
          transaction.nodes.remove(node.id);
      }, { origin: "interaction", commandId: execution.commandId });
    };
  }
  function captureDeleteSelectionInput(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidDeleteInput();
    const selection = value.selection;
    if (!isRecord7(selection))
      throwInvalidDeleteInput();
    const nodeIds = captureSelectionIds(selection.nodeIds, nodeId);
    const edgeIds = captureSelectionIds(selection.edgeIds, edgeId);
    const evidence = captureSnapshot(value.evidence);
    return { selection: { nodeIds, edgeIds }, evidence };
  }
  function captureSelectionIds(value, createId) {
    return captureArray(value, (id) => typeof id === "string" && id.length > 0).map(createId);
  }
  function captureArray(value, isValue) {
    if (!Array.isArray(value))
      throwInvalidDeleteInput();
    const length = +value.length;
    const captured = [];
    for (const index of Array.prototype.keys.call({ length })) {
      const entry = value[index];
      if (!isValue(entry))
        throwInvalidDeleteInput();
      captured.push(entry);
    }
    return captured;
  }
  function throwInvalidDeleteInput() {
    throwInvalidDelete("Delete Selection Command input is malformed.");
  }
  function captureSnapshot(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidDeleteInput();
    const revision = value.revision;
    if (typeof revision !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(revision) || revision < 0)
      throwInvalidDeleteInput();
    const nodes = captureArray(value.nodes, isCanvasNode);
    const edges = captureArray(value.edges, isCanvasEdge);
    return { revision, nodes, edges };
  }
  function requireEvidenceNode(nodes, id) {
    const node = nodes.get(id);
    if (!node)
      throwInvalidDelete("Delete Selection evidence does not contain a selected Node.");
    return node;
  }
  function canonicalIds(ids) {
    return [...new Set(ids)].sort(compareIds5);
  }
  function compareEntities(left, right) {
    return compareIds5(left.id, right.id);
  }
  function compareIds5(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
  }
  function arraysEqual2(left, right) {
    return left.length === right.length && left.every((id, index) => id === right[index]);
  }
  function throwInvalidDelete(message) {
    throw new InteractionError("INVALID_DELETE", message);
  }
  function throwStaleSelection() {
    throw new InteractionError("STALE_SELECTION", "Delete Selection target evidence is stale.");
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/diagnostic-events.js
  var interactionDiagnosticEvents = Object.freeze({
    pointerRejected: "nodebraid.plugin.interaction.pointer.rejected",
    inputRejected: "nodebraid.plugin.interaction.input.rejected",
    gestureCancelled: "nodebraid.plugin.interaction.gesture.cancelled",
    connectionMaterializerFault: "nodebraid.plugin.interaction.connection-materializer.fault",
    commandFault: "nodebraid.plugin.interaction.command.fault"
  });

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/move-nodes-command.js
  var moveNodesCommand = defineCommand("interaction.nodes.move");
  function createMoveNodesHandler(kernel) {
    return (input, execution) => {
      execution.signal.throwIfAborted();
      const moves = captureMoveNodesInput(input);
      if (moves.length === 0) {
        throw new InteractionError("INVALID_MOVE", "Move Nodes Command requires at least one Node.");
      }
      const seenNodeIds = new Set;
      let previousNodeId;
      for (const move of moves) {
        if (seenNodeIds.has(move.nodeId)) {
          throw new InteractionError("INVALID_MOVE", "Move Nodes Command Node IDs must be unique.");
        }
        seenNodeIds.add(move.nodeId);
        if (previousNodeId !== undefined && previousNodeId > move.nodeId) {
          throw new InteractionError("INVALID_MOVE", "Move Nodes Command Node IDs must use canonical order.");
        }
        previousNodeId = move.nodeId;
        if (!Number.isFinite(move.basePosition.x) || !Number.isFinite(move.basePosition.y) || !Number.isFinite(move.position.x) || !Number.isFinite(move.position.y)) {
          throw new InteractionError("INVALID_MOVE", "Move Nodes Command positions must be finite.");
        }
      }
      return kernel.transact((transaction) => {
        for (const move of moves) {
          const node = transaction.query.getNode(move.nodeId);
          if (!node || node.position.x !== move.basePosition.x || node.position.y !== move.basePosition.y) {
            throw new InteractionError("STALE_GESTURE", "Move Nodes Command source position is stale.");
          }
        }
        for (const move of moves) {
          const node = transaction.query.getNode(move.nodeId);
          if (!node)
            throw new InteractionError("STALE_GESTURE", "Move Nodes Command target disappeared.");
          transaction.nodes.replace(move.nodeId, { ...node, position: move.position });
        }
      }, { origin: "interaction", commandId: execution.commandId });
    };
  }
  function captureMoveNodesInput(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidMoveInput();
    const moves = value.moves;
    if (!Array.isArray(moves))
      throwInvalidMoveInput();
    const length = +moves.length;
    const captured = [];
    for (const index of Array.prototype.keys.call({ length })) {
      const move = moves[index];
      if (!isRecord7(move))
        throwInvalidMoveInput();
      const id = move.nodeId;
      if (typeof id !== "string" || id.length === 0)
        throwInvalidMoveInput();
      const basePosition = captureMovePoint(move.basePosition);
      const position = captureMovePoint(move.position);
      captured.push({ nodeId: nodeId(id), basePosition, position });
    }
    return captured;
  }
  function captureMovePoint(value) {
    if (!isRecord7(value))
      throwInvalidMoveInput();
    const x = value.x;
    if (typeof x !== "number")
      throwInvalidMoveInput();
    const y = value.y;
    if (typeof y !== "number")
      throwInvalidMoveInput();
    return { x, y };
  }
  function throwInvalidMoveInput() {
    throw new InteractionError("INVALID_MOVE", "Move Nodes Command input is malformed.");
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/shortcut-value.js
  function defineInteractionShortcut(shortcut) {
    if (typeof shortcut !== "object" || shortcut === null || typeof shortcut.code !== "string" || shortcut.code.length === 0 || !Array.isArray(shortcut.modifierAlternatives) || shortcut.modifierAlternatives.length === 0) {
      throw new InteractionError("INVALID_SHORTCUT", "Interaction Keyboard Shortcut is malformed.");
    }
    const alternatives = shortcut.modifierAlternatives.map((value) => {
      if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || typeof value.alt !== "boolean" || typeof value.control !== "boolean" || typeof value.meta !== "boolean" || typeof value.shift !== "boolean") {
        throw new InteractionError("INVALID_SHORTCUT", "Shortcut modifiers are malformed.");
      }
      return Object.freeze({ alt: value.alt, control: value.control, meta: value.meta, shift: value.shift });
    });
    return Object.freeze({ code: shortcut.code, modifierAlternatives: Object.freeze(alternatives) });
  }

  // packages/plugin-interaction/dist/shortcut-runtime.js
  var preventDefaultDisposition = Object.freeze({ defaultAction: "prevent" });
  function createShortcutRuntime(options) {
    const entries = new Set;
    const entriesByKey = new Map;
    let disposed = false;
    const disposeEntry = (entry) => {
      if (entry.disposePromise)
        return entry.disposePromise;
      let completeDisposal;
      entry.disposePromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
        completeDisposal = resolve;
      });
      for (const execution of entry.executions)
        execution.controller.abort();
      Promise.all([...entry.executions].map((execution) => execution.settled)).then(() => {
        for (const key of entry.keys) {
          if (entriesByKey.get(key) === entry)
            entriesByKey.delete(key);
        }
        entries.delete(entry);
        completeDisposal();
      });
      return entry.disposePromise;
    };
    const service = Object.freeze({
      register(binding) {
        if (disposed)
          throw new InteractionError("SHORTCUT_DISPOSED", "Interaction Shortcut Service is disposed.");
        const accepted = acceptBinding(binding);
        const keys = new Set(accepted.shortcut.modifierAlternatives.map((modifiers) => bindingKey(accepted.shortcut.code, modifiers)));
        for (const key of keys) {
          if (entriesByKey.has(key)) {
